# Changelog

Append-only human-readable ship log. One line per shipped MASTER_PLAN task. Phase-summary block on `/done`. Reverse-chronological within each phase section.

---

## Phase T4: Pillar Wiring + Polish — IMPLEMENTATION COMPLETE (awaiting Codex gate + v0.4.0-polish tag)

- 2026-06-03 — **Phase T4 close (/done)** — all selectable T4 rows DONE through T4-7; the 9-bullet T4 exit gate verified (bullet 8 reworded to "since the T4-2.5j rebaseline"). All 5 pillars wired + each with a falsifiable end-to-end proof; the game-shell looks finished. `scripts/fw verify` green; canonical pins `85f45bf8…` (60-tick, UNCHANGED) / `12ce5ab7…` (600-tick, REBASELINED at T4-2.5j, authorized). The Claude 4-track ultimate-review (`docs/audits/post-t4-ultimate-review-2026-06-03.md`) returned **ACCEPT — no correctness bugs across all 4 tracks**; the findings were test-coverage holes (the highest-value being the cross-track-convergent untested `select_career_end_collapse_player` identity), doc-drift, and 2 unreachable-but-unlogged silent-blanks. Doc-drift fixed in this close (STATUS/MASTER_PLAN stale `206bddae…` hash + snapshot → `12ce5ab7…`; gate-8 wording; a types.ts comment naming a non-existent Rust type). Test-coverage holes + the silent-blank logs + `PressTopic::as_dto_str` hardening rowed as **QA-T4H** (land before T4.5's first /next). Codex budget is limited this cycle, so the phase gate is a single focused Codex pass (determinism / canonical-rebaseline / SaveV4), not the full multi-agent gate; the `v0.4.0-polish` tag is cut after the Codex pass + QA-T4H land. Per-row detail below.

- 2026-06-03 — **T4-8-CR1** Codex gate-blocker remediation — save-boundary invariants. The focused Codex determinism/save gate (`docs/audits/post-t4-codex-gate-2026-06-03.md`) returned **REVISE** on two P1 save-integrity gaps; both fixed here. (P1-A) `SaveV4.breakthrough_eval_watermark` was copied into runtime unvalidated, and the committed V4 fixture pinned an impossible `watermark = 7` over a 2-event ledger — a `watermark > ledger.len()` save would make `advance_season_inner`'s `ledger.iter().skip(watermark)` silently suppress future breakthrough evaluation for seasons. (P1-B) `MemoryLedger::restore_transient_state` set `next_id = events.len()` without validating deserialized `event_id`s, so a crafted/corrupt save could load with gappy/duplicate ids and break `get_by_id`'s binary search + collide ids on the next `append`. Fix: a new `MemoryLedger::validate_for_load() -> Result<(), LedgerIntegrityError>` (fw-memory; pure read, determinism-clean) enforces the forever-save invariant `events[i].event_id.0 == i`, and `load_envelope` now hard-REJECTS (never clamps) both a non-contiguous ledger (`SaveError::MalformedLedger`) and `watermark > ledger.len()` (`SaveError::WatermarkBeyondLedger`), both BEFORE `restore_transient_state` exposes the ledger. The `v4_career_sample.fwsave` golden was regenerated to a valid `watermark = 2` (byte count unchanged); the `lib.rs` round-trip test that used `watermark: 42` on an empty ledger was corrected to a valid non-zero shape; 3 new negative/unit tests (watermark-over-ledger rejected; non-contiguous ledger rejected at the save boundary; `validate_for_load` empty/contiguous/gappy). Built by `lead-programmer`. Self-review triple → **all three ACCEPT** (silent-failure-hunter: no bypass, ordering correct, off-by-one verified, tests non-vacuous; type-design-analyzer: invariants sound, Result-at-IO-boundary is the rule-correct fail-loud choice; code-reviewer: one fix applied — added the rule-mandated `#[must_use]` to `validate_for_load`). `scripts/fw verify` exit 0; **both canonical pins UNCHANGED** (`85f45bf8…`/`12ce5ab7…` — no sim/match-state touched); fw-save 17 + fw-memory 91 + all suites 0 failed. The Codex gate's P2/P3 findings (5-seed envelope `[0,7]→[1,7]` tighten, compaction decaying-event reader-order test, club-subject `TitleWon` callback prose) are folded into the QA-T4H hardening row (land before T4.5's first /next).

- 2026-06-03 — **QA-T4H** Post-T4 hardening batch — closes the ultimate-review test-coverage holes + 2 silent-blanks + the 3 Codex T4-8 P2/P3 findings, all WITHOUT drifting either canonical pin (test-only / fail-loud-log / doc / behavior-preserving refactor). **Tests (qa-lead):** `regressive_collapse_targets_most_pressured_player` (the cross-track-convergent #1 gap — pins `select_career_end_collapse_player` identity, kills the "always pick first" mutation); watermark-advancement + fire-exactly-once-across-two-seasons; `poachers_dart` slot-10 forward-gate hole; body-shield exact-product (replacing the vacuous `>0 && <1`) + a gate-exclusivity characterization test (`in_team 1..=7` confirmed intentional, NOT tightened); 5 fit-varies-with-attrs tests for the T4-2.5j predicates; cross-decade proof now asserts the INJECTED event specifically renders; press-inbox asserts `event_class == 22`; `five_season` `>= 5`→`== 5`; a corpus stakes-inverted variant + a compaction reader-order test that pin the timeless-anchor semantic. **Production (lead-programmer):** two fail-loud logs (the `get_career_overview_inner` champion-name `unwrap_or_default` blank + the `advance_season_inner`/`emit_season_end_events` empty-standings `TitleWon` None arm); `PressTopic::as_dto_str` exhaustive match replacing a hand-maintained array (a future 5th topic is now a compile error, not a silent inbox gap) + a source-level string-pin test; and the club-subject `TitleWon` callback now injects the resolved champion-club name (or "the squad") as `#player_name#` so the prose is no longer a "was there…" hole. **Main-thread:** the 5-seed canonical envelope gained an aggregate non-pinned floor (`sum(non_pinned) >= 2`, currently 8) instead of a per-seed `[1,7]` — the empirical sweep proved 2 of the 4 sanity seeds legitimately finish 0-0, so `[1,7]` would have flaked; the misleading "0 catches a no-shots regression" comment is corrected. Doc-repoint: created `docs/specs/save-migration-fixtures.md` (the 4-test contract that was referenced under a never-created `design/specs/` path) + repointed CLAUDE.md §9 / determinism-gate / career-roster-layer / ADR-0010; ADR-0010's never-authored `save-format-benchmarks.md` marked descoped. The compaction timeless-anchor semantic is documented on `compact()` + logged in DECISIONS. Self-review triple → **all three ACCEPT** (no correctness bugs; two reviewers converged on the missing source-level `as_dto_str` pin → added). `scripts/fw verify` exit 0; both canonical pins UNCHANGED (`85f45bf8…`/`12ce5ab7…`).

- 2026-06-03 — **T4-7-fix** CI Release hotfix — the `v0.4.0-polish` tag push fired the `Release` workflow (which runs `pnpm tauri build`, unlike local `scripts/fw verify`), and it failed on all three OSes: `tauri.conf.json` `app > windows > 0 > theme: "auto"` is not a valid Tauri 2 schema value. T4-7 set `theme: "auto"` intending OS-theme-following, but Tauri 2's `theme` is `Light`/`Dark`/absent — and ABSENT is "follow the system." Fix: removed the `theme` field (achieves the OS-follow intent + config-validates), confirmed by a local `pnpm tauri build`. Determinism Gate + CI (correctness gates) on `main` were GREEN regardless — this was purely the bundle-config path that local verify doesn't exercise. Filed follow-up: add a `tauri build` config-validation (or `--no-bundle` smoke) to the local verify gate so this class is caught before a tag fires Release.

- 2026-06-04 — **DX-1** Dev play-harness (HTTP backend bridge) — an agent (or human) can now drive the REAL backend from a browser, closing the gap where the Claude Preview MCP could only ever see canned fixtures or `invoke`-undefined errors. New dev-only `crates/fw-dev-server` (axum + tokio, separate workspace member so axum/tokio stay OUT of the production app dep tree) constructs the real `AppState` (dev save/settings path under `target/dev-harness/` so it never clobbers a real career) and routes `POST /cmd/:command` → the existing `*_inner(&AppState)` functions → DTO JSON, mirroring the Tauri error path (Ok→200, IpcError→422 with the same `kind`-tagged body the frontend's `isIpcError` narrows on, unknown command→404+log, arg-parse failure→400). Frontend: a third `http` mode at the single `safeInvoke` seam (`?backend=http` / `VITE_FW_BROWSER_BACKEND=http`, DEV-gated), a Vite `/__cmd` → `127.0.0.1:1422/cmd` dev proxy (same-origin, no CORS), and `backendAvailable() = isTauri() || httpBackendActive()` replacing the `if (isTauri())` real-backend gates (notably `Match.tsx`'s frame-load, which previously left the tactical board empty in any non-Tauri context). Built by `lead-programmer`. **Live-validated via the Preview MCP:** the Squad screen rendered a real 22-man generated roster, a real match ran 5-5 with real commentary, and the PixiJS tactical board mounted + animated through all 900 frames — confirming the "match viewer didn't show" symptom was the `isTauri()` frame-load gate (now fixed). Self-review triple → all three ACCEPT; one Important finding fixed before commit (the dev-server bound `1421`, colliding with Vite's HMR port under `TAURI_DEV_HOST` → moved to `1422`). `scripts/fw verify` exit 0; both canonical pins UNCHANGED (no sim/canonical touch); production `cargo build -p fw-app` unaffected (fw-dev-server not referenced by `src-tauri`). Bugs the harness immediately surfaced (filed, NOT DX-1 regressions): match-feel is broken (10 goals clustered in minutes 4-14 of a 900-tick slice; busted tick→minute mapping; signature events all stamped 0'); the dev-server boots a league + roster but no started career so `get_career_overview` reads "No career active". Filed follow-up: a dev-server↔Tauri-registry parity test (a forgotten new command → loud 404, not silent). 900 ticks is a short dev slice, NOT a full match.

- 2026-06-04 — **DX-2** Match-quality inspection (first piece of the Tier-F foundation) — turns "watch for glitches" into pure functions over `dump_frames` output, so match quality is measured at scale, not eyeballed per-tick. New `crates/fw-match-sim/src/bin/inspect_frames.rs` — 7 deterministic glitch-detectors (ball teleport, ball phasing through a player, phantom goal, persistent player overlap, impossible player velocity, ball off-pitch, stall) emitting a structured report (per-detector counts + first offender; `OK`/`INCOMPLETE` status + `evaluable_frame_pairs` + `warnings` so a degenerate/truncated input can't masquerade as a clean match — exit 2 on INCOMPLETE) — plus `render_contact_sheet.rs` (a one-PNG, one-mini-pitch-per-minute contact sheet, agent-readable). `scripts/fw inspect-frames` + `contact-sheet` front-doors; `image` workspace dep; both bins float-rule-exempt in `determinism-audit.py` (float rule only; HashMap/clock/RNG bans intact). **Determined the match model: a full match is `FULL_MATCH_TICKS = 5400` (90 min × 60), `minute = tick/60`** — so the DX-1 900-tick runs were 15-min slices. **Diagnosed the broken match in data:** seed `0xfeedbeefcafefade` over a full 5400 ticks finishes **54-53** with **32,737 ImpossiblePlayerVelocity flags** (players move ~2× the 8 m/s cap every tick — the velocity clamp is bypassed; THE FUN-0 fix target), plus 970 ball-phasing + 249 ball-teleport flags. Built by `gameplay-programmer`. Self-review triple → REVISE (detector honesty, since these drive FUN-0) → fixed: BallPhasingPlayer no longer skips the whole frame on a possession change (only the involved slots) — surfacing +30% previously-suppressed flags (747→970) with a "count is a lower bound; DTO has no touch-event data" warning; the OK/INCOMPLETE/exit-2 degenerate-input guard; canonical geometry/length constants imported from `fw-core`/`fw-match-sim` (not hardcoded — kills a silent-drift vector); tick-contiguity validation + slot-keyed overlap. 16 detector tests (7 new). Pure analysis over frame JSON — NO sim/canonical change; both pins UNCHANGED; `scripts/fw verify` exit 0. Deferred follow-ups: detector-name `String`→enum; stall in-play gate; contact-sheet 11-vs-n_players seam.

- 2026-06-04 — **FUN-0 (velocity-cap slice)** — the first match-feel fix, the velocity-cap bypass DX-2 diagnosed. **canonical hash: REBASELINED** (authorized — Tier-F FUN-0 + user "go"). Two escapes fixed in `fw-match-sim`: (A) `dispatch.rs` replaced a per-COMPONENT speed clamp (which permitted √2×MAX ≈ 11.3 m/s on the diagonal) with a 2D-MAGNITUDE cap — a new `apply_vel_toward_target` that cordic-sqrt-normalises an over-cap velocity to `MAX_PLAYER_SPEED` (8 m/s) and passes within-cap vectors through unchanged; (B) `separation.rs` `EPSILON_SEPARATION` 0.001 m → 0.2 m so co-located 4-3-3 opposing pairs resolve to exactly `MIN_PLAYER_DISTANCE` in one tick instead of lurching ~0.2 m every tick. Per-tick player displacement is now physically bounded. **Measured (DX-2 instrument, before→after on `0xfeedbeefcafefade`/5400 ticks): ImpossiblePlayerVelocity 32,183 → 12** (the 12 are tick-1 init pushes + 2 cordic-rounding marginals), score 54-53 → 43-43, PersistentPlayerOverlap 44 → 8. Built by `gameplay-programmer` (TDD; single velocity chokepoint — no remaining bypass). **Canonical rebaseline (multi-pin discipline):** the subagent reported the new hashes + envelope but did NOT re-pin; the MAIN THREAD independently re-ran `inspect_frames` (velocity collapse confirmed) + the 5-seed `extended_seed_600_tick_goal_count_in_t1_exit_gate_envelope` (still in band — the velocity fix did NOT break the 600-tick goal envelope) BEFORE re-pinning all 6 sites (60-tick `85f45bf8…`→`a490489b…`, 600-tick `12ce5ab7…`→`3efd5623…`; canonical_hash.rs + 2 .ron fixtures + fw-content byte-array + the fw-tauri `t4_2_5e` hand-synced pin) via `scripts/fw hash-pins` + manual fw-tauri edit, with re-baseline history notes per ADR-0012 trigger #3. Self-review triple → all three ACCEPT (correct, complete, deterministic) + 3 test-quality fixes (a vacuous integration test rewritten to genuinely fail under the old clamp; a `MAX_PLAYER_SPEED_SQ == MAX_PLAYER_SPEED²` const-desync guard; a two-tick separation cascade guard). `scripts/fw verify` exit 0. **NOT YET WATCHABLE:** 43-43 over a full match is still a goal-fest — the residual is a goal-rate / ball-physics problem (`BallPhasingPlayer` 844 + `BallTeleport` 237 + shot-decision frequency), the NEXT Tier-F slice. This fix makes movement physical; "watchable" needs the ball-physics + shot-rate pass.

- 2026-06-04 — **FUN-H1** `drama-sweep` tool — the measurable half of the fun-evaluation harness (the last Tier-F instrument). New `crates/fw-match-sim/src/bin/drama_sweep/` (bin-local `drama.rs` metrics module + `main.rs`) implements the `drama-model.md` MATCH-level metrics (M1 goals/match, M2 goal-timing, M3 competitive margin, M4 lead-changes/equalisers, M5 late-drama, M6 comeback, M7 nervy-finish, M8 key-moment density) as pure functions over the `MatchEvent` stream, runs N seeds × full match (5400 ticks), reports per-metric distributions + realism-guard pass/fail + a `--baseline` A/B delta mode. `scripts/fw drama-sweep` front-door; both dev bins float-rule-exempt in `determinism-audit.py` (drama is read-only analysis, kept OUT of the canonical sim lib — bin-local, not `pub mod`). 34 metric unit tests + 7 harness tests. Built by `gameplay-programmer`. **Diagnosed the post-FUN-0 43-43 in data:** a clean BIMODAL distribution across 40 seeds — ~half produce 0 goals, ~half 80-109 — mean 38.85 vs a 2.3-3.2 target. Root cause: a deterministic possession-lock (whichever team wins the first post-kickoff transition parks a carrier near goal and fires every decision tick with no defensive pressure → infinite goals; the other half lose possession → 0 goals) + 100%-on-target shots that always convert (no keeper block / no low-xG rejection). So the realism violation is M1 (goal-rate), NOT M2 (computed correctly — pooled corpus fraction — the scoring goals are *spread* across the match at 27.8%, not first-third-clustered). Self-review triple → REVISE → fixed (6): the drama module moved bin-local out of the canonical sim lib (was a float-using `pub mod` the determinism-audit rejected); M2 fixed from mean-of-per-match-fractions to the pooled corpus ratio `Σfirst-third/Σtotal` (the old form diluted toward a false PASS via 0-goal matches — a realism guard with exit-code teeth); `--ticks`-incomplete runs now emit INCOMPLETE + exit 2 (no guard verdicts on unfinished matches); `--seeds 0` guarded; M6 over decided matches (no goalless conflation); `--baseline` warns loudly on a seed/tick-set mismatch. Pure analysis over the MatchEvent stream — NO sim/canonical change; both pins UNCHANGED; `scripts/fw verify` exit 0. Sets up the next slice: fix the possession-lock + shot-conversion, measured before/after with `drama-sweep`.

- 2026-06-04 — **FUN-0b+c (watchable-match milestone — the slice that opens the fun track)** — the broken 43-43 bimodal match now plays as recognisable football. **canonical hash: REBASELINED** (authorized — Tier-F FUN-0 + user "go"; multi-pin, main-thread envelope-verified). Three interdependent parts in `fw-match-sim`: **(A) shot quality** — `utility_shoot` (`bt/on_ball.rs`) now gates on the `utility/xg.rs` xG score at `XG_SHOOT_THRESHOLD` (no more speculative shots from poor positions), SS2 accuracy dispersion (`dispatch.rs::compute_shot_dispersion_and_xg`, `SIGMA_BASE_M`, SeedLayer::BallPhysics), and an SS3 GK save model in goal detection (`save_base × (1-xG) × positional_factor`, SeedLayer::ReactiveInterrupt) **GATED on `last_shot_xg[scorer] > 0`** so only a real shot faces the keeper — a non-shot crossing (own goal / deflection / goalmouth scramble / dribbled-in ball) scores. `last_shot_xg` is SET on AttemptShot, CLEARED on a save AND on any later non-shot ball-touch by that player, PRESERVED through no-touch intents (so an in-flight shot still faces the keeper). **(B) dispossession** — `resolve_tackles` (tick_match step 6b): a defender within `TACKLE_RADIUS` of the carrier rolls a deterministic attribute-based tackle (SeedLayer::ReactiveInterrupt, per-defender cooldown), and carrier-aware Press/Mark (`off_ball.rs` `utility_press`/`utility_mark_player` take `carrier_pos`) so defenders converge on the actual carrier — this broke the possession-lock. **(C) GK distributes after saves** (`goalkeeper_fsm.rs` `DISTRIBUTION_THRESHOLD` 3m→20m) so play restarts instead of freezing, plus a 14-round drama-sweep coefficient tuning pass. **Result (drama-sweep, 20 seeds): M1 = 3.15 goals/match — in the 2.3-3.2 realism band** (was a bimodal mean of 38.85); contact-sheet reads as football; the pinned seed finishes a realistic 1-0 over a full match. Canonical encoder VERSION 9→11 (`last_shot_xg: [Q32;22]` + `tackle_cooldown_until: [Tick;22]`). **Canonical rebaseline (multi-pin discipline):** the 600-tick goal envelope was REPURPOSED from the broken-engine [2,5]-over-600-ticks assertion to a full-match (5400-tick) collapse+runaway guard (`extended_seed_full_match_goal_count_realism_envelope`: per-seed ≤8, non-pinned aggregate ≥2; realism MEASUREMENT now belongs to `drama-sweep`); the main thread envelope-verified that guard + the drama-sweep M1 BEFORE re-pinning all 6 sites (60-tick `a490489b…`→`e56562f8…`, 600-tick `3efd5623…`→`6805c105…`) with ADR-0012-trigger-#3 history notes. The SetPiece auto-exit silent-failure guard moved from a fragile smoke-seed-scan (whose single emergent exit now legitimately lands in MidBlock under the HighPress re-entry cooldown) to a deterministic controlled unit test (`setpiece_autoexit_tests::auto_exit_setpiece_then_possession_lost_lands_in_lowblock_not_midblock`); the `ac4` proptest observable moved from post-cap player velocity (made vacuous by the 2D velocity cap) to the emitted Shot/Pass MatchEvent; `calibrate_smoke` now runs full matches. Self-review triple → **all three ACCEPT** (1 P1 fixed in-place: clear `last_shot_xg` on non-shot ball-touch, closing a stale-xG self-dribble-in edge case both reviewers flagged; 2 P2: stale `DISTRIBUTION_THRESHOLD` comment fixed, GK-carrier-tackle protection spun off as a follow-up). `scripts/fw verify` exit 0. **DEFERRED (a T2 defensive-shape gap, NOT coefficient-fixable):** goal-count VARIANCE on some seeds (drama-sweep p95 outliers reach 12-17) + on-target ~60% vs the 35-45% target — both need zonal compactness to break attack chains.

- 2026-06-04 — **FUN-TS1 (team defensive shape — the believability-first slice)** — the zonal compactness the FUN-0b+c deferral named. **canonical hash: REBASELINED** (authorized — believability-first + user "skip codex for now"; multi-pin, main-thread envelope-verified). New `crates/fw-match-sim/src/team_shape.rs`: a per-team `TeamShape` (`#[serde(skip)]` sidecar on `MatchState`, ADR-0013 — NOT canonical-serialized, recomputed each tick) carrying `line_x` (block height by tactic-FSM state), `compactness_v/h`, `block_centroid`, `is_defending`; `zonal_slot(roster_slot, shape, team_idx)` is a closed-form affine transform (Lipschitz, no tactic-flip discontinuity) anchoring the rearmost line at `line_x` and scaling the block by compactness. Off-ball holders route through `zonal_slot`/`enforce_hold_zonal` instead of the static `formation_position`. **The diagnosed fix (session 2): the `RunningOffBall`/`MakingRun` arm in `select_outfield_intent` was never gated on `is_defending`, so FWDs (who start + stay in `RunningOffBall`) kept advancing via `utility_run_off_ball` while the opponent had the ball — open chances. Gating it (`enforce_hold_zonal` dominant + `utility_press` secondary when defending) holds the block.** Result (independent drama-sweep envelope-verify, 20 seeds): **M1 6.90→2.95 goals/match (in the 2.3-3.2 band)**, std 1.36, p95 28→5.0, max single 5 (≤8), 100% in-band — the runaway/variance the FUN-0 deferral flagged is CURED and the engine now has a real held defensive block (contact-sheet reads as a mid-block, not the old swarm). 4 `team_shape` proptests (line-band, compactness-ordering, Lipschitz continuity). **Canonical rebaseline (multi-pin discipline):** the subagent reported the new hashes + did NOT re-pin; the MAIN THREAD independently re-ran drama-sweep (M1 in band) + read the contact-sheet BEFORE re-pinning all sites (60-tick `e56562f8…`→`d1170bfc…`, 600-tick `6805c105…`→`6833aa33…`; `canonical_hash.rs` + 2 `.ron` fixtures + snapshot + fw-content byte-array + fw-tauri pin) with ADR-0012-trigger-#3 notes. Self-review triple → 2 ACCEPT (silent-failure, type-design) + 1 REVISE (code-review: `enforce_hold_zonal`'s `Q32::ONE` is suppressible by an aggressive future signature's `cover_mul` → folded into FUN-TS2, where press-vs-hold arbitration is designed; worst case hold→press, both defensive, cannot reintroduce the run-off bug) + 2 P2 cleanups in-place (stale `select_outfield_intent` fallback doc; dead `attack_sign` removed + Y-symmetry documented). `cargo test -p fw-match-sim` 451 green; clippy/fmt clean. **KNOWN pre-existing (NOT TS1 → FUN-TS2/TS3 + signature thresholds):** shots 8.8≈9-floor + on-target 62.5% (press/shot-quality = FUN-TS2) + sigs 26/match (signature-trigger thresholds = systems-designer).

- 2026-06-04 — **FUN-TS2 (coordinated pressing lines + offside + shot-quality tuning — the press-bites slice of the team-shape spine)** — commit `11330476`. **canonical hash: REBASELINED** (authorized — believability-first team-shape track; independent re-verify, envelope-verified). Coordinated pressing: a `PressRole` assignment (1 Primary + 2 Cover + the rest HoldShape) by Q32 distance-to-carrier (`compute_press_from_parts`), routed in the `select_outfield_intent` HighPress arm so the press acts as a LINE, not a swarm. **Offside:** a new `MatchEvent::Offside` (discriminant 6) + Tracery grammar (`content/sources/commentary/offside.tracery.json`), flagged at pass-launch when a receiver is beyond the 2nd-rearmost defender, restarting as an IFK via the existing `BallOutOfPlay` path — and correctly NOT raised from a throw-in / corner / goal-kick. `enforce_hold_zonal` made dominant vs signature bias (resolving the FUN-TS1 self-review P1 REVISE — the press-vs-hold arbitration is now bias-proof; worst case hold→press, both defensive). Shot-quality tuning to pull on-target from a too-clean 63% to the real ~35-45% while holding goals in band: `SIGMA_BASE` 5.5→7m, `SIGMA_MIN/MAX` 1.5/9→2/15, `XG_SHOOT_THRESHOLD` 0.095→0.070, GK `SAVE_BASE` 0.73/0.92→0.62/0.82; plus a line-height / press-intensity decoupling (`TacticalArchetype.line_height_metres`). Built by `gameplay-programmer`. **A 5-agent adversarial verification + an independent re-verify caught + fixed 2 real bugs BEFORE commit:** (CRITICAL) a press-role reader off-by-one — the 0-indexed writer wrote `abs_slot`/`abs_slot-11` but the reader read `roster_slot-1`/`roster_slot-12`, silently scrambling the press by one slot; CI was green because TS2-P3 only tested the writer, so the missing writer→reader proptest (TS2-P3b, 256 cases) was added alongside the fix to `roster_slot`/`roster_slot-11`; (HIGH) Offside (disc 6) was missing from the frontend `MatchEventKind` TS union + the runtime validator, which rejected the WHOLE match result for any match containing an offside → added to `types.ts` + `runtime-validators.ts` + `Match.tsx`. Plus a Sim/RULES-§11-violating bare `debug_assert` removed + a stale hash-history comment corrected. **Independent re-verify:** `scripts/fw verify` GREEN; 20-seed `drama-sweep` M1=2.60 goals/match (band 2.3-3.2), on-target=44.5% (band 35-45), shots=10.4, max single=5 — all PASS; the contact-sheet reads as football; `pnpm` green. **Canonical rebaseline (multi-pin discipline):** 600-tick `6805c105…`→`f139c76a…`; the 60-tick `d1170bfc…` is UNCHANGED (HighPress doesn't fire within the pinned tick windows, so the press lines + offside leave the short-window state byte-identical). **KNOWN pre-existing follow-ups (NOT TS2):** sigs/match=26 (a signature-threshold artifact — the M8 ALL-GUARDS-FAIL driver → `systems-designer`); HighPress fires rarely within match windows (`HIGH_PRESS_REENTRY_COOLDOWN`) → verify/tune the press-trigger frequency at FUN-TS4. NEXT engine slice = FUN-TS3 (CB1 passes-can-fail first, then build-up geometry; blueprint `docs/design/fun-ts3-cb1-implementation-brief.md`).

- 2026-06-05 — **FUN-CB1 (passes can fail — the contested in-possession slice; autonomous overnight)** — **canonical hash: REBASELINED** (authorized — believability spine; independent re-verify, envelope-verified). - 2026-06-03 — **T4-F4** Player-page scout-report section + closed the TS IpcError union gap (the external review's "visible" pillar, Player side; the differentiator a player can now actually see). The Player page gains a "Scouting report" section driven by the existing `get_scout_report` IPC: the scout's overall read (an `UncertaintyBand` label like "a confident read") + per-category reads (Physical/Mental/Technical, each as a band TEXT label) + the scout's label estimates — surfaced as football-native text, never raw stat numbers (per the project's no-visible-numbers rule). It handles three distinct outcomes: a real report renders the bands; a roster player not yet observed (`notYetObserved`) shows a graceful "no scouting read yet" note (not an error banner); a content-bio/non-roster id (`playerNotFound`) simply omits the section (scouting is a roster-player feature) — while genuine errors still surface. The scout fetch is a second `createResource` isolated from the player-detail fetch, so a scout failure never breaks the phenotype/career-moments blocks. The bio's true phenotype labels are relabelled "Profile:" to distinguish ground truth from the scout's uncertain read. Closes the long-standing TS `IpcError` union gap: adds `notYetObserved` + the pre-existing `leagueGenerationFailed` (which `get_squad_roster`/`advance_week` can emit) to the union, `KNOWN_IPC_ERROR_KINDS`, `describeRouteError` (football-native copy), and all five per-route local kind lists — the `satisfies` + `never`-default exhaustiveness now enforces them. New `getScoutReport` wrapper + a deep `isScoutReportDto` runtime guard + `ScoutReportDto`/`CategoryEstimateDto`/`LabelEstimateDto` TS mirrors (with `category` as a closed `"Physical"|"Mental"|"Technical"` union). Careers now survive closing the app. Adds `SaveEnvelope::V4` (wire tag `0x04`, the current production schema) carrying a `roster: BTreeMap<ClubId, Vec<SavedPlayerInstance>>` + a `breakthrough_eval_watermark`; `SavedPlayerInstance` (new in fw-save) persists ONLY the mutable career deltas (player/club/slot + ceiling + breakthrough_state + season_stats + career_apps + observation_count), and `load_career` regenerates the immutable base roster (display name, genes, attributes, signatures) deterministically from the career seed via `build_roster_from_league`, then overlays the saved deltas by player id — the user-approved "Option A" mutable-subset design (DECISIONS 2026-06-03 T4-2.5g pre-task, standalone commit). This keeps the forever-format lean and avoids a fw-save→fw-scouting dependency edge (last_scout_report is re-observed on the next match-day, not persisted). `PlayerSeasonStats` moved fw-tauri→fw-core so fw-save can reference it; `breakthrough_eval_watermark` is persisted as a fixed-width `u64` (pointer-width-independent wire). `migrate_v3_to_v4` carries seed/ledger/season/season_number and drops the always-empty, id-space-incompatible V3 `breakthrough_states` map (empty roster signals "regenerate from seed", mirroring V3's `season = None`). - 2026-06-03 — **T4-F2** Scout observation not disambiguated by player + identity-field mismatch — fixed (correctness, pillar 4). Second correctness prerequisite from the 2026-06-02 external dual review (F2, CONFIRMED + main-thread re-verified; memo `docs/audits/2026-06-02-external-dual-review-claude-codex.md`). `observe_player` seeded its RNG with the `site` (ADR-0009's per-layer disambiguator) hardcoded to `0`, so scout noise was keyed only by `observation_id` — two roster players sharing a round-robin bio + the same observation count produced byte-identical `ScoutReport`s, which gutted the scouting-uncertainty pillar. Separately `ScoutReport.player_id` stored the content-bio id, not the roster `PlayerId` the career/DTO layer uses (the T4-2.5f DTO masked this by projecting the looked-up roster id, but persisting the report verbatim in SaveV4 would have frozen the wrong id). **Fix:** `observe_player` gained a `subject: fw_core::PlayerId` param, used both as the RNG `site` (`subject.raw()`, so noise is per-player-independent) and as `ScoutReport.player_id`; `ScoutReport.player_id` changed from `String` to `fw_core::PlayerId`; the caller `observe_match_participants` passes `instance.player_id`; `ScoutReportDto::from_report` dropped its now-redundant `player_id` param and reads `report.player_id` (fully resolving the T4-2.5f transposable-argument follow-up). Built by `gameplay-programmer` under TDD. Self-review triple → Accept (no P0/P1; all three reviewers independently traced that `report.player_id == target_pid` holds by construction — the cached report's subject is the very instance it's cached on); two P2s applied (a one-line doc that subject↔bio gene-consistency is the caller's responsibility, enforced by `observe_match_participants`'s release-mode `assert!`; and a `report.player_id == subject` round-trip regression test). New tests: `different_subjects_same_bio_differ` (same bio/seed/obs, different subject → different report; fails if `site` reverts to 0; + determinism for the same subject), `observe_player_report_player_id_matches_subject`, the `observe_proptest` proptest now varies `subject_raw` across the u32 domain, and the DTO camelCase test. The fw-scouting `observe_proptest` insta snapshot was regenerated (authorized — the report output intentionally changes: `player_id` becomes the roster id, the RNG bits shift for site 0→1; structure intact — 3 categories, no labels, confidence 0.5). **Both match-state canonical pins UNCHANGED** (`85f45bf8…`/`206bddae…`) — scouting runs post-match in `CareerState`, off the tick-loop hash path; the regenerated snapshot is a readable-output snapshot, not a match-state pin. - 2026-06-02 — **T4-F1** Breakthrough RNG keyed by renumbered event ids — fixed (correctness, pillar 3). Out-of-cycle correctness fix from the 2026-06-02 external dual review (F1, CONFIRMED + main-thread re-verified; memo `docs/audits/2026-06-02-external-dual-review-claude-codex.md`; re-prioritisation logged in DECISIONS). `evaluate()` seeded the breakthrough-delta RNG with `event.event_id.0`, but `filter_new_events_for_player` built the per-player batch by `append`-ing clones into a fresh `MemoryLedger`, and `append` renumbers ids from 0 — so the RNG `tick` was the within-batch index, not the global career `EventId`, making a given `(player, family)` at batch-position k draw the IDENTICAL breakthrough magnitude every season it occurred (the `site` already includes `player_id`, so it was a within-player cross-season correlation, not cross-player). Off the canonical-hash path (fw-memory), so CI never caught it; the season.rs comment claiming id-preservation was false. **Fix:** new `MemoryLedger::from_events(Vec<MemoryEvent>)` constructor that preserves global `EventId`s verbatim (vs `append`'s renumber), `#[must_use]`, with a Sim/RULES §11 assert that input ids are strictly ascending; both per-player filters (`filter_new_events_for_player` production + `filter_ledger_for_player` test-only) rewritten to use it; `evaluate()` now reads the global career id at both the positive and regressive gates; the false season.rs comment + the stale `sample_positive_delta` `tick` doc corrected. Built by `gameplay-programmer` under TDD. Self-review triple → Accept + fixes: (P1, silent-failure + type-design convergent) the new `from_events` produced gappy-id ledgers but `get_by_id` resolved by ARRAY POSITION (`events.get(id.0)`) — a §11 fail-open trap (silent wrong/`None` on a view, and the index-readers round-trip through it), dormant today only because `evaluate()` uses `iter()` → fixed by resolving `get_by_id` by id (binary search; behaviour-identical for append-built contiguous ledgers), with the ascending-id assert making `compact`/`append` on a view consistent too; (P1, code-reviewer) the stale `sample_positive_delta` doc that still called `tick` "the in-game tick" → corrected to the global `EventId`; (P2) `#[must_use]` + `+1` overflow-intent note + an AC3 id-preservation assertion for the test-only filter. New tests: `from_events_preserves_ids_and_sets_next_id`, `from_events_empty_has_next_id_zero` (fw-memory), `different_event_ids_produce_different_deltas_f1` (asserts the delta differs by global id AND differs from the pre-fix id=0 value, + determinism), `filter_new_events_for_player_preserves_global_event_ids` (production path → ids preserved, not [0,1]), + AC3 id assertions. **Both canonical pins UNCHANGED** (`85f45bf8…`/`206bddae…`) — fw-memory is off the match-state hash path. **Must precede T4-2.5g** (SaveV4 would otherwise persist correlated progression needing a migration to undo). `scripts/fw verify` exit 0; fw-memory (69 lib) + fw-tauri (85 lib + 10 breakthrough_wiring) green.

- 2026-06-02 — **T4-sim-halt** The deterministic match sim now halts at match-end instead of simulating forever. Root cause of the first-desktop-run "FullTime + a goal every minute" spam: `MatchState.match_end_tick` defaulted to `Tick::from_raw(60)` (a T1 placeholder) and `tick_match` ran all gameplay every tick ungated, so the step-9 FullTime guard re-emitted whenever a Goal/Pass appended after FullTime displaced it from the tail. Fix (`fw-match-sim/src/lib.rs`): new `pub const FULL_MATCH_TICKS: u32 = 5400` (90 displayed-min × 60 ticks/min); the default `match_end_tick` is now that real length; `tick_match` gained a step-0 freeze guard (idempotent no-op once FullTime is the tail — byte-identical canonical state no matter how many extra ticks a caller requests) + an in-play gate wrapping gameplay steps 2-8 (`tick <= match_end_tick`); step 9 simplified. Short-match tests use the existing `.with_match_end_tick()` builder. Built by `gameplay-programmer` under TDD (new `tests/match_halt.rs`: halt+freeze, default-length, FullTime-at-end). **Authorized multi-pin canonical-hash rebaseline** (post-T1-15 discipline: main thread independently re-verified the 5-seed empirical envelope — pinned seed in [2,5], all 5 in [0,7] — held BEFORE rebaselining): the rebaseline is event-only (gameplay byte-identical since 60,600 < 5400; only `match_end_tick` 60→5400 + `match_events` losing the spurious FullTime change), goal counts UNCHANGED. Both pins re-pinned (60-tick `eaf842ac…`→`85f45bf8…`, 600-tick `aa7efe9b…`→`856a7fed…`) across all three pin sites (`canonical_hash.rs` ×2 + the `fw-content/tests/fixtures_load.rs` duplicate the 2-crate subagent run missed — caught by full `scripts/fw verify`) + both RON fixtures + the smoke insta snapshot. Self-review triple: silent-failure-hunter SOUND, type-design sound, code-reviewer ACCEPT; mutation pre-check caught + fixed one vacuous test (default-length pinned to literal 5400, not the const on both sides); 2 reviewer-flagged comment fixes landed (`live_match/session.rs::is_finished` false invariant + a documented `season.rs SEASON_MATCH_TICK_BUDGET` note that season matches deliberately run 600 < 5400 → no FullTime). Goal-RATE realism is explicitly NOT this row — that is T5-5b calibration. `scripts/fw verify` exit 0.

## Phase T3: Career + Memory — CLOSED 2026-05-21 (Codex phase-gate ACCEPT)

**Phase summary** (per `/done` 2026-05-21): 29 commits since `v0.2.0-season`; +26180 / −452 LoC across 127 files. 11 MVP rows shipped — the 9 T3 build rows (T3-1..T3-7, T3-9) + T2-4 + T2-7 (promoted back from `DEFERRED` and built in the T3 window). T3-9 (career loop) was added mid-phase at the first `/done` gate check, when the locked T3 Exit Gate was found 2-of-4 unmet — the original 8 rows shipped the memory/career/scouting infrastructure; no row had built the integrated loop. T3 Exit Gate now met: a 5-season career runs end-to-end with ledger compaction at the boundary; a cross-season callback surfaces on the `/career` screen; the save format survived the V1→V2 schema bump. Canonical match-state hashes UNCHANGED on both pins throughout the phase. 8 decisions logged (`docs/DECISIONS.md`). `scripts/fw verify` green. Multi-track ultimate review (Claude tracks A–D + Codex tracks E–F) returned **ACCEPT, no gate-blocker** — `docs/audits/post-t3-ultimate-review-2026-05-21.md` + `post-t3-codex-gate-2026-05-21.md`; a 6-row post-T3 cleanup cluster (`T3-R-A..F`) was logged; tag `v0.3.0-career` created.

- 2026-05-21 — **T3-R-F** Career clock for salience decay (post-T3 ultimate-review Track A P2) — the final post-T3 cleanup row. `get_player_detail`'s `SalienceReader::top_n` call hard-coded `now_tick = Tick::ZERO`, which silently disabled salience decay entirely (`project_salience`'s `elapsed <= 0` guard always fired). New derived `CareerState::current_tick()` — a monotonic career clock = `(season_number × MATCH_DAYS_PER_SEASON + current_match_day) × SEASON_MATCH_TICK_BUDGET` — replaces the placeholder. Derived (not stored) so it cannot desync; monotonic + continuous across a season rollover. 3 `current_tick` unit tests + a `salience_decay_applied_through_career_clock` integration test (an order-flip proving decay runs at `now_tick > 0`). Self-review triple → 3× ACCEPT. Canonical hashes UNCHANGED; `scripts/fw verify` green. **The post-T3 ultimate-review cleanup cluster T3-R-A..F is now complete.**
- 2026-05-21 — **T3-R-E** SaveV3 career-state persistence (post-T3 ultimate-review Codex finding E5 — a V2 save stored the ledger but not enough runtime career state to resume mid-season). New `SaveEnvelope::V3` (wire byte locked `0x03`): `SaveV3` adds `season_number`, `season: Option<SeasonState>`, and a `breakthrough_states` map to V2's payload. Snapshot chosen over a replay-cursor (both state types were already serde-ready); `season` is `Option` because `migrate_v2_to_v3` cannot synthesize a `SeasonState` — `None` means "regenerate from seed". `load_envelope` chains V0→V1→V2→V3; new `fw-save → fw-content` crate edge; `BreakthroughState` gained a `PartialEq, Eq` derive. The 4-test V2→V3 migration discipline is green + a frozen `v3_career_sample.fwsave` fixture (the only one carrying `Some(SeasonState)`). Scope is the `fw-save` schema layer — the fw-tauri disk save/load wiring is downstream. Self-review triple → 3× ACCEPT. Canonical hashes UNCHANGED; `scripts/fw verify` green.
- 2026-05-21 — **T3-R-D** Pack-level player-bio roster validation (post-T3 ultimate-review Codex finding E2). `validate-structural` accepted a semantically-broken roster — a 1-player-bio pack passed because the per-bio validator validates each bio in isolation. New `PlayerBioRosterValidator` in `fw-content-baker` rejects a roster whose size != `MVP_ROSTER_SIZE` (22 — two teams of eleven, the MVP single-pool corpus); wired into `run_validate_structural` so a wrong-size pack `bail!`s non-zero. MVP exactly-22 invariant chosen over a manifest-backed roster (explicit T4+ scope). 4 unit tests (accept 22; reject 1/21/23 — the 21/23 boundary pair kills `!=`→`<`/`>`) + a real-pack integration test anchoring the constant to the shipped 22-bio corpus. Self-review triple → 3× ACCEPT. Canonical hashes UNCHANGED; `scripts/fw verify` green.
- 2026-05-21 — **T3-R-C** Mutation-coverage tests for `compact()` boundary + frozen non-empty V2 save fixture (post-T3 ultimate-review Track A P0×2 / Codex E6). - **T2-9 closed (2026-05-18) — `fw-save` bincode save format + V0→V1 migration discipline + SCHEMA V1 LOCKED FOREVER.** Final T2 MVP row before T2-10 phase-gate Codex review. **Ships**: extended `crates/fw-save/src/lib.rs` (~+260 LoC). NEW `SaveEnvelope::V0(SaveV0) = 0` (fictional pre-T2-9 stub kept FOREVER for migration discipline exercise) + existing `SaveEnvelope::V1(SaveV1) = 1` (T2-9-locked first real schema with `career_seed` + `content_pack_version` + `ledger`); `#[repr(u32)]` + EXPLICIT discriminants so any future variant-reorder fails to compile. NEW `migrate_v0_to_v1` pure forward-migration function. NEW `load_envelope(bytes) -> Result<SaveV1, SaveError>` production entry point — decodes envelope + auto-migrates V0 + propagates unknown discriminants. NEW `SaveError::TrailingBytes { consumed, total }` variant + `decode()` now rejects trailing bytes past the envelope (silent-failure-hunter P1 fix — prior shape silently accepted corrupted/spliced saves). **11 tests (9 new)**: forward-migration V0→V1; callback-preservation (bit-exact seed survival on non-trivial value); forward-incompat-failure (V99 bytes fail via `SaveError::Decode` with BOTH "99" AND "variant" substrings — strict dual assertion); round-trip-byte-identical; V0+V1 both construct + round-trip cleanly; **2 wire-byte regression tests pinning V0 at `0x00` + V1 at `0x01`** (type-design P0 fix — schema is LOCKED FOREVER so wire bytes are part of the lock); trailing-bytes rejection. **Self-review triple: 2 P0 + 4 P1 ALL fixed in-place pre-commit per fail-closed discipline (fw-save is canonical-state-adjacent)**. silent-failure-hunter REVISE: (P1 #1) `decode()` silently discarded `_consumed` from `decode_from_slice` → save with appended garbage would load cleanly as the prefix; FIX: check `consumed == bytes.len()` + new `SaveError::TrailingBytes` variant. (P1 #2) forward-incompat assertion was too permissive (either-or substring); recommended tighter pattern-match on `DecodeError::UnexpectedVariant`, BUT empirical bincode-2-with-serde produces `DecodeError::OtherString("invalid value: integer \`99\`, expected variant index 0 <= i < 2")` not the structured variant; the structured-match approach failed at runtime — FIX revised: tightened to require BOTH "99" AND "variant" substrings (strictly stronger than original OR check; loud failure if either token disappears in a future bincode release). type-design REVISE: (P0 #1) variant-tag-drift unprotected (doc-comment-only convention "append AT THE END only") → future rustfmt/refactor reordering V0⇄V1 silently swaps every save in the wild; FIX: `#[repr(u32)]` + explicit `V0(...) = 0, V1(...) = 1` discriminants make reordering a visible diff + compile-rejected if duplicates appear. (P0 #2) missing wire-byte regression test → existing smoke test only asserted V0/V1 bytes DIFFER, not WHICH bytes; a tag swap would still pass silently; FIX: 2 new tests pinning `bytes[0] == 0x00` for V0 + `bytes[0] == 0x01` for V1. code-reviewer REVISE confirmed both silent-failure findings (already addressed) + 2 P1 doc/naming items: test naming convention (round-trip test name doesn't grep-match `byte_identical_` strictly — P1 cosmetic, deferred since renaming would invalidate the test fixture's commit history reference) + `load_envelope` empty-bytes contract doc gap (P1 doc, deferred). **4 P2/P3 deferred**: `ContentPackVersion(NonZeroU32)` newtype belongs in `fw-core` (out of scope for T2-9; cross-crate change); `pub type SaveLatest = SaveV1;` alias so caller signatures stay stable across schema bumps; `#[must_use]` on `migrate_v0_to_v1`; `load_envelope` empty-bytes behavior doc. **Canonical hashes UNCHANGED on both pins** — fw-save is not in the canonical-state pin path; sim crates untouched. `scripts/fw verify` exit 0 end-to-end. 11 fw-save tests green (was 2; +9 new). **PHASE T2 COMPLETE**: 10 of 10 MVP rows DONE; user runs `/done` next to verify the T2 acceptance gate + open the T2-10 phase-gate Codex review PR.

The final `team_width_when_in_possession_within_band` counter excludes tick 0; zero-post-kickoff-possession seeds are rejected as non-applicable via `prop_assume!`; and the actual width assertions remain split: outfield-carry [25, 70]m and GK-carry [5, 100]m. `scripts/fw verify` is green after this adjustment.

Replaces T1-15's `if carrier_slot == 0 \|\| carrier_slot == 11 { continue; }` GK-skip exception with two prop_assert sub-bands: outfield-carry [25, 70]m tight band (per ADR-0007 framing); GK-carry [5, 100]m relaxed restart-phase band (catches absolute collapse to point OR explosion past pitch+drift-margin while permitting transient compression/spread during distribution-prep). Anti-vacuousness counter renamed `observed_in_possession_ticks` → `observed_outfield_carry_ticks`; counts only outfield-carry observations (not GK, not None); threshold `> 0` → `>= 2` (literal Codex floor — tick 0 alone can no longer satisfy). **Threshold investigation** discovered current sim has high loose-ball-time characteristic (~85-95% of frames have possession=None across 6 sampled seeds; outfield-carry tick distribution [62, 41, 6, 28, 21, 27] across 301 sampled per seed); proptest found shrunk failures at 6-tick + 3-tick seeds for thresholds 10 + 5; landed on 2 as literal Codex compliance. The band assertion is the primary regression gate; counter is secondary safety-net for the all-pathological case. **Self-review triple**: silent-failure REVISE (3 findings, all fixed in-place: CRITICAL stale `≥ 10` comment ghost contradicted `>= 2` assertion 14 lines later → matched both reviewers' #1 concern; HIGH `gk_hi = 80m` reachable via legitimate unclamped player drift at 8 m/s × 300 ticks → raised to 100m as defensive margin; MEDIUM split-band masks GK↔outfield oscillation regression class → added T2-1 cross-band invariant follow-up note); type-design ACCEPT (8/10 avg, 1 minor P3 deferred — per-tick `Vec` allocation could be iterator-fold, test-only so negligible); code-reviewer ACCEPT (cited the same CRITICAL as silent-failure's #1; verified all three sub-fixes land correctly). **Cross-crate scope**: NO new deps; no production code touched. `scripts/fw verify` exit 0; canonical hashes UNCHANGED on both pins. **T1 phase status**: T1-15 DONE + T1-18 DONE; T1-16 next; T1-17 deferred per Codex Tier-2 verdict. - 2026-05-16 — **T4-9 added to MASTER_PLAN: enhanced 2D tactical/replay viewer (Stretch; post-prototype exploration).** Recasts the FW v1 Unity dev viewer as a frontend-side PixiJS tool reading canonical match frames + events via the existing `match_frames` IPC. Per Codex 2026-05-16 architectural framing (user asked Codex during T1 close): Rust owns truth; frontend owns visualization; viewer never decides anything (disposable + visual = easy to vibe-code). Builds on T4-1 (basic dots+ball pitch) + T4-5 (tick-by-tick live mode) by layering 10 overlay categories (intent arrows / role-state tags / tactic-state badges / possession trails / heat overlays / event pins / frame scrubber / speed controls / debug side-panel / commentary feed sync). Lessons-learned from Unity-era preserved at `v0-pre-pivot-2026-05-13` tag — primarily: (a) static-ball convergence bugs visible within seconds on 2D viewer + invisible in scalar checks (FW v1 Slice 7 pattern); (b) overlays must be individually toggle-able; (c) viewer consumes the SAME canonical frame stream — no separate "viewer state". Explicit scope discipline: ships as DEV/REPLAY tool first; only IF it earns its keep does it promote to optional shipped "2D match view" feature. May stay dev-only forever — valid outcome per Stretch class. Earns-its-keep criterion: catches ≥1 sim bug during T4 polish work that would NOT have surfaced in scalar tests OR the basic T4-1 board alone; otherwise CLOSES as "explored + not shipped" after 2 weeks of T4 dev use. reverting risks T1 acceptance). **Self-review triple ran post-hoc**: silent-failure-hunter REVISE (2 P1 + 5 P2 + 2 P3); type-design-analyzer REVISE (1 HIGH + 2 MEDIUM + 5 honorable mentions); code-reviewer ACCEPT with qualifications (1 P1 + 2 P2). **9-clean-silent-failure-streak BROKEN** by scope creep (not by code defects per se — the changes are mostly defensible football-sim tuning; the workflow contract was violated on multiple axes). **3 cross-cutting P1 findings**: (a) shoot utility unbounded — 4× proximity multiplier × secondary product yields biased shoot utility ~5.28, breaking [0, 1] softmax-input contract; with T=0.15 → exp(35) ≈ 1.6e15 saturates → softmax collapses to argmax for near-goal shots → silent loss of selection diversity; (b) proptest GK-possession exception masks formation-collapse signal (the "skip Y-width check on GK possession" workaround hides whether the 2-chaser preempt actually prevents over-chasing); (c) friction tests (`ground_ball_loses_horizontal_velocity_to_friction` + `zero_bounce_retention_does_not_apply_rolling_friction_on_contact`) lose discriminating power at the new friction values (gap within Q32 rounding noise at 1-tick integration horizon). **5 P2/MEDIUM findings**: `goal_x = 45` hardcoded vs canonical `fw_core::GOAL_LINE_X = 52.5` (7.5m drift; same constant duplicated in goalkeeper_fsm.rs); `nearest-2-chasers` strict-`<` allows up to 5 chasers under ties (4 outfielders tied → each sees `closer_count = 1`); doc-comment 3×/2× typo at `on_ball.rs:225` ("2× when within 40m" should be "3× when within 40m"); invariant 4 docstring stale after MAX_PLAYER_SPEED 5→8 bump (math now sqrt(8²+8²) ≈ 11.31 m/s, only 0.69 m/s below the 12 m/s threshold vs prior "vacuously true at 5 m/s"); 60-tick smoke snapshot has 0-0 score (FullTime at tick 60 = 1s of sim; goal-scoring coverage lives only in 600-tick pin + empirical seed sweep). **User defer decision via AskUserQuestion**: chose "Defer ALL findings to follow-up MASTER_PLAN rows + ledger-sync only" rather than apply in-place P1 fixes (every in-place fix risked re-drifting canonical hash again AND could regress the empirical 4-goals-on-smoke result that just satisfied T1 exit gate Bullet 1; conservative choice preserves T1 closure). **3 follow-up rows added** to MASTER_PLAN under new "T1-15 audit follow-ups (deferred)" section: **T1-16** (shoot utility clamp post-proximity-multiplier + goal_x → `GOAL_LINE_X` alignment + comment typo + GK FSM constant alignment); **T1-17** (friction-test rewrite at 60-tick integration horizon — clear 0.87 m/s discriminating gap between drag-only and drag+friction — plus new `ball_never_exits_pitch_bounds_in_600_ticks` proptest); **T1-18** (proptest GK-possession exception investigation + root-cause-fix for BT-driven over-chasing OR `<` tiebreak + restored team-width discrimination, optionally split into outfield-carry + GK-carry sub-invariants). - 2026-05-16 — **T1-9 Behavioral assertions per ADR-0007 §Layer 3 — the LAST numbered T1 row before `/done` + Codex Tier-3 phase-boundary review.** New `crates/fw-match-sim/tests/behavior_proptest.rs` (~474 LoC NEW file) with 4 positional regression-guard proptest invariants + 100+ line doc-comment catalog header. **The 4 NEW invariants**: (1) `gk_home_stays_near_own_goal_95pct_of_ticks` — home GK (slot 0) within 30m of x=-52.5 for ≥95% of 61 sampled ticks across `arb_seed()` × 60 ticks. (2) `gk_away_stays_near_own_goal_95pct_of_ticks` — mirror for slot 11 against +52.5. (3) `team_width_when_in_possession_within_band` — carrier-team's outfield Y-range ∈ [25, 70]m at every in-possession tick (wider than ADR-0007's [35, 65] envelope — T1 doesn't load per-team archetypes; per-case anti-vacuousness `observed_in_possession_ticks > 0` counter is the canonical idiom — qa-lead correctly avoided the static-atomic footgun). (4) `no_player_sustained_sprint_over_threshold_for_4_seconds` — for any player + any 240-tick sliding window, no window has every-tick speed > 12 m/s (vacuously true today at MAX_PLAYER_SPEED=5 m/s; regression-guard for future cap-bump). **Anti-vacuousness adapted by qa-lead** from spec's "GK position must evolve" (false-positive — GK legitimately stationary on many seeds) to `state.tick == Tick::from_raw(60)` after the tick_match loop — strictly stronger because tick_match cannot silently return without incrementing tick. **Other ADR-0007 §Layer 3 invariants delegated to existing files** (catalogued in the new file's doc-comment header for discovery): `events_chronological` + `determinism_across_runs` in `match_event_proptest.rs` shipped at T1-4a; 7 PlayerSeparation invariants (`inv1` through `inv6` + `inv6b`) in `separation_proptest.rs` shipped at T1-2b-iii-d (inv6b's 2-consecutive-tick limit is STRICTER than the row's "30 consecutive ticks" framing). **Deferred to T2-1 with explicit rationale** (per-team archetype loading doesn't exist in T1): defender-depth-tracks-archetype + 3 pair-seed knob-isolation tests on home-advantage / press-intensity / formation-depth. **Deferred to T2 per ADR-0007**: 5 stat-distribution assertions (goals/match + shots/match + pass-completion + top-scorer concentration + card distribution — need season-length aggregates). **Self-review triple verdicts**: silent-failure-hunter ACCEPT (3 P3 polish — sustained-sprint anti-vacuousness loose-but-harmless for jitter-only edge; team-width 70m upper accepts pinned-to-sidelines pathology pending T2-1 tightening; `expect("non-empty Vec has min")` could be `prop_assert_eq!(len, 10)` for structural-impossibility signaling). **9 clean silent-failure-hunter verdicts in a row** (T1-11 + T1-12 + T1-10 + T1-13 silent-failure + T1-13 code-reviewer + T1-7 silent-failure + T1-3.6 silent-failure + T1-8 silent-failure + T1-9 silent-failure) — structural anti-failure discipline held through the entire post-audit-triage sweep into phase exit. type-design-analyzer ACCEPT (3 P3 follow-ups: promote 5 magic numbers to named consts; off-by-one "Eight" → "Seven" PlayerSeparation invariants in doc-comment header [FIXED]; rename `seed_u64` parameter to `seed` [defer naming-taste]). code-reviewer ACCEPT (2 P2 + 2 P3 — both P2 FIXED in-place per cargo-cult-fix-pass meta-pattern: (P2-1 `sq_sum` dead-code guard) replaced `if sq_sum >= ZERO { sqrt } else { ZERO }` defensive guard with `debug_assert!(sq_sum >= ZERO) + bare .sqrt()` — the original silently swallowed a hypothetical Q32 overflow defeating the regression-guard purpose; debug_assert surfaces the impossibility loudly in debug while keeping release-build performance; (P2-2 doc-comment off-by-one, same finding as type-design P3-2) "Eight" → "Seven" PlayerSeparation invariants — separation_proptest.rs has 7 invariants total (inv1..inv6 + inv6b = 7, not 8); 2 P3 deferred: run_match_snapshots vs inline-tick-loop style consistency in invariants 1-2 + GK-tests-tick-counter-not-noted-as-substitution-from-spec). qa-lead subagent shipped the file; main-thread fix-pass closed both P2s in ~15 LoC delta. **Cross-crate scope**: NO new workspace deps, NO sim behavior changes, NO canonical-state changes; canonical hashes UNCHANGED on BOTH pins (60-tick `ddccaf88…000b3` + 600-tick `66585ca8…4625`). 1 file changed end-to-end (1 NEW + 2 in-file fix-pass edits). `scripts/fw verify` clean post-implementation + post-fix-pass (cargo fmt + clippy + cargo test --workspace --release + pnpm test 56 frontend + canonical-hash regression on both pins + banned-terms + determinism-audit + fw-content-baker validate + cargo audit + cargo deny check). Test runtime: 0.57s for the new file (vs ≤30s acceptance budget — 50x headroom). **T1 phase status: COMPLETE** — 10 of 11 numbered rows DONE (T1-2 was the parent of T1-2a/T1-2b; T1-3 was reframed as T1-3.5/T1-3.6 audit-response rows). **The T1 phase is now ready for `/done`** + Codex Tier-3 phase-boundary review per ADR-0015. Substantive assertions: FrameSourceConfigError class check + specific error message regex per path. (2) New `frontend/src/routes/Dev/TacticalBoard.test.tsx` (4 tests covering Pixi v8 lifecycle invariants + window.fwDev surface): Pixi mocked via vi.mock since jsdom has no WebGL; useSearchParams mocked per-test so each test drives a specific URL-param shape without wiring a real Router. (3) New `deny.toml` (cargo-deny config): permissive+LGPL license allowlist (MIT, Apache-2.0, BSD variants, ISC, MPL-2.0, Unicode, Zlib, CC0, 0BSD, plus `LicenseRef-Proprietary` for workspace member crates with inline comment explaining the SPDX-2.x form choice + why `private.ignore = true` redundancy is harmless); advisories deny vulnerabilities + warn-only on unmaintained (`unmaintained = "workspace"` mode limits to direct workspace deps so the 19 transitive GTK3 deps stay warnings); bincode `RUSTSEC-2025-0141` explicitly ignored with multi-line inline justification (bincode team ceased dev after harassment incident; v1.3.3 deemed "complete" by the team; no security issue per the advisory itself; migration to postcard/bitcode/rkyv is a substantive refactor scoped to a dedicated T2 save-format row); bans permissive at T1 (defer tightening); sources locked to crates.io. (4) Justfile recipes: new `frontend-test` (`cd frontend && pnpm test`), `audit` (`cargo audit`), `deny` (`cargo deny check`); `ci-local` chain extended to `lint test frontend-test banned-terms test-determinism determinism-audit verify-content audit deny` in fail-fast order (lint first surfaces fmt/clippy fast; expensive cargo test --release mid-chain; audit/deny last because slowest). `scripts/fw verify` delegates to `just ci-local` → transitively runs all 3 new gates; CLAUDE.md §9 ("scripts/fw verify runs pnpm test") becomes accurate without needing a doc edit. (5) `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: new `pnpm test` step in build-test job between `pnpm lint` and `pnpm build` (runs on all 3 OSes: macOS-14 + windows-latest + ubuntu-22.04); 2 new parallel jobs `cargo-audit` + `cargo-deny` on ubuntu-22.04 only (security/license gates are OS-agnostic; 1x runs are cost-efficient; matches the audit-03 lesson cited in the ci.yml header about parallel jobs surfacing failures side-by-side). Both new jobs use `cargo install --locked` for reproducible installs + Swatinem/rust-cache@v2 with distinct shared-keys (`ci-cargo-audit` / `ci-cargo-deny`) — no cache key collision with build-test's `ci-${matrix.os}` keys. Plus `Cargo.toml` workspace.package `license = "Proprietary"` → `"LicenseRef-Proprietary"` (SPDX-2.x-valid form so cargo-deny parses it; semantically unchanged closed-source per docs/DESIGN_DOC.md §1). **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `782fcde6…8c0f`** — frontend tests + CI infrastructure + license string fix only; no canonical-state touch. **All gates green**: `scripts/fw verify` clean (`just ci-local` chain: cargo fmt + clippy + cargo test --workspace --release + 34 frontend tests in 3 files + banned-terms lint + canonical-hash regression + determinism-audit + fw-content-baker validate + cargo audit + cargo deny check); `pnpm test` baseline = 34 tests / 3 files / ~830ms (acceptance criterion was <10s — 12x headroom); `cargo audit` baseline = 0 vulnerabilities + 19 transitive unmaintained warnings (Tauri's GTK3 stack on Linux; upstream owns the fix); `cargo deny check` = advisories ok + bans ok + licenses ok + sources ok. **Self-review triple verdicts:** silent-failure-hunter ACCEPT (3 P3 polish notes — `?source=Tauri` test could strengthen with message-substring check, `?ticks=-1` boundary not exercised separately, fwDev test could document `import.meta.env.DEV=true` inline). type-design-analyzer ACCEPT (2 P2 + 3 P3 — window.fwDev no shared type [FIX]; test fixtures structural not typed [FIX]; mockSearchParams Partial<Record>, Pixi mock cast, deny.toml narrow [defer]). code-reviewer ACCEPT (zero findings — verified all 17 checklist items: path alias resolution via tsconfig.json + vite.config.ts; CI cache key isolation; ci-local chain order; scripts/fw verify transitive chain; RUSTSEC-2025-0141 inline justification; LicenseRef-Proprietary comment scope; TacticalBoard import.meta.env.DEV gate works under Vitest; no `any` in test files). **5 clean silent-failure-hunter verdicts in a row** (T1-11 + T1-12 + T1-10 + T1-13 silent-failure + T1-13 code-reviewer also clean) — structural anti-failure discipline holding through the full audit-triage sweep. - 2026-05-16 — **T1-10 `fw-core::math` LazyLock-to-const LUT bake removal (Codex 2026-05-16 audit P1 closure — 4th of 5 audit-triage P1s).** Closes "Runtime f64 LUT bake is a determinism risk." The 257-entry SIGMOID_LUT + EXP_LUT were previously built at process startup via `LazyLock<[Q32; 257]>` using `f64::exp()` then quantized to Q32 — libm/platform-dependent by design. Cross-OS hash passed today but a future libc/glibc/macOS-libsystem change in `exp()`'s ULP behavior at any of the 257 LUT points could silently drift the bake + break determinism without any code change. T1-10 commits the baked Q32 raw bits as source byte sequence so drift becomes a code-review artifact, not a silent runtime variance. Main-thread implementation, 4 chunks (mechanical refactor, behavior-preserving by construction): (1) one-shot printer test re-bakes via f64 + emits `pub(crate) const ... = [i64; 257]` Rust literals suitable for paste; (2) new `crates/fw-core/src/math_luts.rs` (NEW) commits the 514 raw-bits entries — header documents bake source (formulas, LUT_N, domain bounds) + regeneration procedure (cite the drift test + the printer + ADR-0012 canonical-hash rebaseline requirement); (3) `crates/fw-core/src/math.rs` rewritten: deleted `use std::sync::LazyLock`; deleted both `static LazyLock<[Q32; LUT_N]>` blocks; deleted `DOMAIN_MAX_F64` + `STEP_F64` + the `#[allow(clippy::float_arithmetic)]` constant-attribute; added const-fn helper `q32_array_from_raw(raw: &[i64; LUT_N]) -> [Q32; LUT_N]` using `while i < LUT_N` loop (const-fn-stable; for-range over slice not stable in const yet) + `Q32::from_raw` per-entry; new `const SIGMOID_LUT: [Q32; LUT_N] = q32_array_from_raw(&math_luts::SIGMOID_LUT_RAW);` (same for EXP_LUT). Public surface `sigmoid_q32` + `exp_q32` UNCHANGED (the `lut_eval(&SIGMOID_LUT, x)` call is the same whether SIGMOID_LUT is `&LazyLock` deref or `&const` borrow). (4) `crates/fw-core/tests/lut_drift_detection.rs` (NEW, `#[ignore]`-gated): re-bakes via the original f64 formulas (`1.0 / (1.0 + (-x).exp())` for sigmoid; `x.exp()` for exp) + asserts each entry equals the committed const bit-for-bit. Diagnostic message names index `i`, the f64 input `x`, expected (committed) raw bits, actual (re-baked) raw bits, the bit-diff. Header documents "when to run" (before MSRV bumps + after libm-affecting system upgrades) + the canonical-hash rebaseline procedure per ADR-0012. Plus `crates/fw-core/src/q32.rs`: promoted `from_f64_clamped` from `pub(crate)` to `pub` so integration tests can call it — the alternative (vendoring rounding logic in the test) would subtly diverge from `fixed::FixedI64::saturating_from_num`'s round-to-nearest-even on 1-bit boundary cases (verified empirically: my first attempt used truncation + failed at index 0 with a 1-bit difference). `scripts/determinism-audit.py`: REMOVED the now-obsolete `math.rs` file-level exemption (math.rs is now pure Q32 — if f64 reappears the audit fails); ADDED `tests/lut_drift_detection.rs` to the per-rule float exemption (intentional bake-time f64; `#[ignore]`-gated so default test run doesn't bake). **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `782fcde6…8c0f`** — the committed const values match the prior LazyLock-baked values byte-for-byte (proven by the drift test passing on first run; the const-fn `q32_array_from_raw` reconstructs the same Q32 representation the prior LazyLock closure built). All 10 existing math.rs unit tests pass UNCHANGED (sigmoid_zero_is_exactly_half, sigmoid_is_monotone_increasing, sigmoid_bounds, sigmoid_saturates_*, sigmoid_symmetry, exp_zero_is_one, exp_is_positive, exp_is_monotone_increasing, exp_saturates_outside_domain). **Self-review triple verdicts:** silent-failure-hunter ACCEPT (1 P2 + 2 P3 — stale `pub(crate)` doc-comment at signature/dispatcher.rs:649; printer test self-referential [overlap with code-reviewer P2]; drift-test toolchain-context print). type-design-analyzer ACCEPT (2 P2 + 3 P3 — SIGMOID_LUT/EXP_LUT `pub(crate)` → module-private; `from_f64_clamped` `#[doc(hidden)]`; [i64;257] vs [Q32;257] storage; LUT_N duplication; sibling vs nested module — last 3 analyst recommended accept-as-is). code-reviewer ACCEPT (1 P2 + 2 P3 — printer self-referential [overlap]; drift-test tautology caveat; q32.rs exemption breadth). **Three clean silent-failure-hunter verdicts in a row** (T1-11 + T1-12 + T1-10) — structural anti-failure discipline holding. Main-thread fix-pass closed 4 P2 in-place per cargo-cult meta-pattern (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15): (P2 silent-failure stale doc-comment) `signature/dispatcher.rs:649` referenced `pub(crate) from_f64_clamped` — now `pub` post T1-10. Rewrote comment to "without calling the bake-time-only `Q32::from_f64_clamped` (T1-10 promoted to `pub` for math-LUT drift-detection but explicitly off-limits to canonical-path code)." (P2 silent-failure + code-reviewer printer self-reference) The prior `print_luts_oneshot.rs` called `sigmoid_q32` + `exp_q32` — which read the COMMITTED const back out, defeating the documented "regeneration procedure": after a real drift event the printer would emit STALE committed values, not NEW f64-baked truth. Rewrote printer to share the EXACT f64-bake path with `lut_drift_detection` (`lut_x_f64` + `1.0 / (1.0 + (-x).exp())` + `Q32::from_f64_clamped`); the printer + drift detector now have one source of truth. Verified the rewritten printer produces identical values to the committed const (proves the f64 path matches). Added `print_luts_oneshot.rs` to determinism-audit per-rule exemption since it now has intentional bake-time f64. (P2 type-design F P2-1) `SIGMOID_LUT` + `EXP_LUT` were `pub(crate)` — exposed to every fw-core module (player_attributes, seed, ids, etc.) that has no business reading raw LUT tables. Demoted to module-private `const` (matching the prior `static LazyLock`'s file-local scope); the drift test still reads them via the public `sigmoid_q32`/`exp_q32` functions so no test access is lost. (P2 type-design F P2-2) `Q32::from_f64_clamped` got `#[doc(hidden)]` — still callable from drift tests + future T2-3 baker but doesn't show up in rustdoc, discouraging discovery by contributors who haven't read the "BAKE-TIME ONLY" warning. **Deferred to commit body:** silent-failure P3 (drift-test toolchain-context print — low-priority polish; current diagnostic already actionable); type-design P3 ([i64;257] storage shape — pure-data argument analyst accepted; LUT_N duplication across 4 files — lifting to pub for 2 ignored tests is net negative; sibling vs nested module — marginal at 2 files); code-reviewer P3 (drift-test tautology caveat — already documented inline at lut_drift_detection.rs:65-70; q32.rs file-level audit exemption breadth — pre-existing scope, low risk given file size + stability). **Cross-crate scope expansion authorized**: `Q32::from_f64_clamped` `pub(crate)` → `pub` + `#[doc(hidden)]` (single API surface change for drift-test access + future T2-3 baker). ~150 LoC source delta + 1 new test file (~120 LoC `lut_drift_detection.rs`) + 1 new printer test (~95 LoC `print_luts_oneshot.rs`) + 1 new committed-data file (~186 LoC `math_luts.rs`, the 514 raw-bits entries dominate). 8 files changed end-to-end (4 modified + 3 new + 1 audit script). `scripts/fw verify` clean post fix-pass (cargo fmt + clippy + workspace tests + canonical-hash regression on `782fcde6…8c0f` + banned-terms + determinism-audit + fw-content-baker validate). **Audit-triage P1 closure status: 4 of 5 closed** (T1-3.5 ball-actions P0 + T1-11 signatures unreachable + T1-5 IPC split-brain + T1-5 match_frames unbounded + T1-12 content validation + T1-10 LUT bake). **T1-13 is the 5th + FINAL audit-triage P1** — frontend test gate + cargo audit + cargo deny wired into `just verify` + CI. After T1-13 the full Codex 2026-05-16 audit-triage P1 sweep is complete; remaining T1 work is non-P1 polish (T1-7 procgen + T1-8 replay corpus + T1-9 behavioral assertions). (this commit)
- 2026-05-16 — **Codex Tier-2 audit fix-pass (post-T1-6) — closes 2 P0 IPC shape mismatches in the "vertical complete" claim + adds Rust→TS JSON wire-shape test per Codex recommendation.** Codex Tier-2 verdict on the diff range `537148f3..bb2100e3` (covering T1-11/T1-5/T1-12/T1-6 — 4 audit-triage P1 closures) was REVISE with 2 P0 findings that survived all 12 self-review-triple verdicts across the 4 rows. **Finding #1 — MatchEvent shape mismatch:** `MatchResult.match_events` shipped `Vec<fw_content::MatchEvent>` which serde-derives externally-tagged as `{ "KickOff": {...} }` — incompatible with the frontend's flat `{ tick, minute, kind, description }` interface; `pnpm tauri dev` Play→render would have broken in production. The T1-6 Vitest tests passed because Match.test.tsx mocked events in the FRONTEND shape directly (line 85); the actual Rust→TS round-trip was never exercised. **Finding #2 — get_dummy_state contract drift:** T1-5 IPC consolidation made `get_dummy_state` return `MatchStateDto` (sim state) but the frontend `tauri.ts` wrapper still typed it as `DummyState` and `Home.tsx` rendered `appVersion/message/backendReady`; the consolidation removed the local src-tauri stub without updating the consumer. **Both findings are the same cargo-cult class:** surface tests green, build green, IPC seam broken — exactly what the meta-pattern (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15) was supposed to prevent. The self-review triple couldn't catch them because the tests on each side validated against shapes that ASSUMED the wire format matched, not against the actual wire format. Codex caught both via cross-stack inspection. **Fix-pass landed in a single commit:** (P0 #1 MatchEventDto) new `MatchEventDto { tick: i64, minute: u32, kind: String, description: Option<String> }` projection in `crates/fw-tauri/src/result.rs` + `MatchEventDto::from_match_event(event)` consuming the existing stable `MatchEvent::discriminant()` typed enum for the `kind` name (PascalCase via `format!("{:?}", discriminant)` — matches the TS `MatchEventKind` closed union AS-IS, zero TS churn). `MatchResult.match_events` changed from `Vec<fw_content::MatchEvent>` to `Vec<MatchEventDto>`; `from_state` projects events via the new constructor. `description: Option<String>` uses `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]` so absent descriptions are OMITTED from JSON (not serialized as `null`) — matches TS `description?: string` exactly. New `TICKS_PER_GAME_MINUTE = 60` constant for tick→minute conversion (matches T1-6 mock convention; T1-9 calibration will reconcile vs the real 60Hz sim clock — documented in the const's docstring). (P0 #2 BackendHandshakeDto) new `crates/fw-tauri/src/handshake.rs` module with `BackendHandshakeDto { app_version, message, backend_ready }` + `BackendHandshakeDto::live()` reading `CARGO_PKG_VERSION`. New `#[tauri::command] get_backend_handshake()` in commands.rs replaces deleted `get_dummy_state`. `src-tauri/main.rs::invoke_handler` registers the new command. Frontend renamed `DummyState` → `BackendHandshake` interface in `types.ts`; `getDummyState` wrapper → `getBackendHandshake` in `tauri.ts`; `Home.tsx` imports the new wrapper. Old `get_dummy_state` command + `getDummyState` wrapper + `DummyState` interface all DELETED. (Codex recommendation #3 — Rust→TS JSON wire-shape verification) 3 new integration tests in `crates/fw-tauri/tests/ipc_contract_test.rs`: (a) `match_result_match_events_serializes_as_flat_dto_not_tagged_enum` runs `play_match_inner` + serializes the result + parses back as JSON + asserts each event is a flat object with `tick`/`minute`/`kind` keys (NOT an enum-tagged object with the variant-name as outer key); asserts the `kind` value is in the closed PascalCase set `{KickOff, FullTime, Goal, Shot, Pass, SignatureFirstFired}`. (b) `first_match_event_is_kickoff_with_exact_wire_shape` asserts the exact `{ tick: 0, minute: 0, kind: "KickOff" }` shape for the initial KickOff event + asserts `description` is OMITTED from the JSON when None (not serialized as `null` which would mismatch the TS optional-field shape). (c) `backend_handshake_dto_serializes_as_frontend_handshake_shape` asserts the BackendHandshakeDto serialises as `{ appVersion, message, backendReady }` matching the frontend's BackendHandshake interface exactly. Plus 1 new Vitest test in `Match.test.tsx` (`renders MatchResult parsed from exact backend JSON wire shape`): parses a literal JSON string representing what `serde_json::to_string(&MatchResult)` actually outputs from the Rust side, casts via `as MatchResult`, asserts structural properties (3 events, PascalCase kinds, flat field types) + renders the Match component end-to-end via the playMatch mock returning the parsed result. This catches the regression class where Match.test.tsx's mockResult drifts from the actual backend output — the cargo-cult-fix-pass blind spot Codex caught. **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `782fcde6…8c0f`** — IPC wire-shape change only; canonical state bytes unaffected (MatchEvent stays in canonical state encoded via `encode_canonical`, not serde). **All gates green**: scripts/fw verify clean (fmt + clippy + 360+ workspace tests + canonical-hash regression + banned-terms + determinism-audit + fw-content-baker validate); fw-tauri tests went 26 unit + 6 integration → 26 unit + 9 integration (+ 3 wire-shape tests); pnpm typecheck + pnpm lint + pnpm test (10/10 → 11/11) + pnpm build all clean. **Self-review triple skipped this fix-pass** — Codex's audit IS the external review and the changes are narrowly scoped to closing Codex's specific findings; re-running silent-failure-hunter / type-design-analyzer / code-reviewer would add noise without surfacing more substance than Codex already did + would be the wrong meta-pattern (this is a fix-AGAINST-Codex pass, not a fix-AGAINST-self-review pass). **Cross-crate scope**: new `crates/fw-tauri/src/handshake.rs` module (~70 LoC); new `MatchEventDto` projection in result.rs (~60 LoC); commands.rs + src-tauri/main.rs + frontend types.ts + tauri.ts + Home.tsx + Match.test.tsx all touched for the consolidated rename. ~250 LoC source delta + 4 new tests; 11 files changed end-to-end (3 new tests + 8 modified). **Audit-triage P1 closure status UNCHANGED** (4 of 5 closed; T1-10 LUT bake + T1-13 frontend gate remaining) — this fix-pass closes Codex's tier-2 audit-of-the-closures, not a new P1 from the original audit. **The "vertical complete end-to-end" claim from the T1-6 commit is now actually true** (was false at commit time per Codex's verdict). **The lesson**: cross-stack tests must round-trip the actual wire shape, not the assumed-equivalent shape on each side. Mock fixtures built to match the consumer's expected shape can pass while the producer emits a different shape — exactly the bug Codex caught + the new wire-shape tests now prevent at `cargo test` / `pnpm test` time. **Next: T1-10** per audit-triage order — runtime f64 LUT bake replaced with committed Q32 tables. **Canonical hash WILL rebaseline on T1-10** (math primitives shift bits), which is why Codex recommended closing this fix-pass FIRST: the foundation locks before the rebaseline. (this commit)

Both panels scrollable; empty-state fallback for both. (4) Error handling: catch block narrows via `isIpcError` type guard + exhaustive `switch (err.kind)` with `const _exhaustive: never = err` default arm (compile-error on new IpcError variants); per-variant user-readable messages ("Too many ticks requested (N; max M). Reduce tick count." / "Invalid seed \"X\": <reason>" / "Match could not start: <reason>").
