Browser-preview mock fallback for `!isTauri()` returns a 2-1 MatchResult with realistic event timeline (KickOff at 0', Goal at 9', Shot at 27', HalfTime at 45', Goal at 54', Goal at 75', FullTime at 90') so minute-marker + badge rendering is exercised in `pnpm dev`. **Vitest substrate activated** — new `vitest.config.ts` (jsdom + solid plugin), `frontend/src/test-setup.ts` (jest-dom global setup), `frontend/src/routes/Match.test.tsx` (271 LoC, 10 tests: renders Play button + no scoreline pre-Play + scoreline after Play + event list with minute markers + busy-state disable + re-enable + IpcError tooManyFrames/invalidSeed/matchInitFailed per-variant formatting + dev-board toggle on/off + seed validation rejects non-hex). 4 new devDeps: `@solidjs/testing-library ^0.8.10` + `@testing-library/user-event ^14.6.1` + `@testing-library/jest-dom ^6.9.1` + `jsdom ^29.1.1`. `pnpm test` previously exited 1 ("no test files"); now exits 0 with 10/10 passing. **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `782fcde6…8c0f`** — frontend-only row; backend IPC contract from T1-5 consumed unchanged. **Self-review triple verdicts:** silent-failure-hunter REVISE (1 P1 + 2 P2 + 2 P3 — tickCount silent fallback to DEFAULT on NaN input; dev-board label drift implying it rendered the recap's seed; "HT" misnomer reading as Half-Time in football vocabulary; eventLabel default arm returning raw kind string; preview-mode visually indistinguishable from real result). type-design-analyzer ACCEPT+P2 (1 P1 + 2 P2 + 2 P3 — `MatchEvent.kind: MatchEventKind | string` forward-compat escape hatch defeated exhaustiveness in eventLabel/badgeClass switches; `isIpcError` structurally accepted any object with `kind: string` field bypassing compile-time exhaustiveness at runtime; tickToMinute branded type deferred to T1-9; HT label drift; dev-board toggle struct consolidation deferred to T4-1). code-reviewer ACCEPT (1 P1 + 1 P2 — dev-board reuse ignores last-run seed/ticks substantively because Dev/TacticalBoard reads useSearchParams + falls back to default 60-tick deadbeef sample; `lazy` imported in a separate `from "solid-js"` statement violating `no-duplicate-imports` lint). Main-thread fix-pass closed 3 P1 + 3 P2 + 1 P3 in-place per cargo-cult-fix-pass meta-pattern (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15): (P1 silent-failure tickCount) prior memo `Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : DEFAULT_TICK_COUNT` returned DEFAULT on `parseInt("abc")` = NaN → `ticksValid()` silently true → Play not disabled → 900-tick match ran with user's invalid input silently ignored. Rewrote memo to return `Number.NaN` on empty/invalid; `ticksValid` checks `Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 && n <= MAX_FRAMES_PER_REQUEST`; onPlay handler explicit NaN guard with clearer error message ("Tick count must be a number between 1 and 7200."). The aria-invalid + disabled-Play path now flips for invalid ticks the same way it does for malformed seed. (P1 type-design + silent-failure eventLabel) `MatchEvent.kind: MatchEventKind | string` forward-compat escape hatch widened the exhaustive `switch (kind)` in `eventLabel` + `badgeClass` to accept any string → unknown variants silently rendered raw kind in badges. Dropped `| string` from `MatchEvent.kind` in `lib/types.ts` (now closed union); rewrote `eventLabel` + `badgeClass` to take `MatchEventKind` not `string` with `const _exhaustive: never = kind` default arms; extracted `isGoal(kind: MatchEventKind)` typed helper replacing the prior `GOAL_KINDS: ReadonlySet<string>` set. Adding a new sim variant in `fw-content::event::MatchEvent` now fails to compile across the entire UI surface — surfacing the drift loudly instead of silently rendering raw kind strings in badges. (P1 silent-failure + code-reviewer dev-board label) prior "Seed: X | Ticks: Y" label alongside the dev-board toggle implied the rendered board matched the recap's seed — but inline DevTacticalBoard reads `useSearchParams()` from the router and falls back to the default 60-tick `0xdeadbeef` sample (no mechanism to push seed/ticks props into a route component). Rewrote label to "Recap: X (Y ticks) · board shows default sample (T4-1 wires last-run props)" — explicit caveat italicized; removes the silent mismatch; real fix deferred to T4-1 when dot rendering lands + the route component can be extracted into a prop-driven `FrameDrivenBoard` component. (P2 silent-failure + type-design "HT") prior Scoreline rendered `HT <seedHex>` next to the score — "HT" in football means Half-Time, so reading "HT 0xfeedbeef..." implied the half-time score was that hex string. Renamed prefix to "Seed:" — explicit + football-friendly + non-confusing. (P2 type-design isIpcError) prior guard accepted any object with `kind: string` field as IpcError → backend variant the frontend doesn't know about would pass + fall through `formatIpcError`'s default arm + bypass the compile-time `_exhaustive: never` check at runtime (the assignment of `err` narrowed-to-`never` succeeded only because the loose guard let any kind value through). Added `KNOWN_IPC_ERROR_KINDS = new Set(["tooManyFrames", "invalidSeed", "matchInitFailed"] as const) satisfies ReadonlySet<IpcError["kind"]>` runtime literal-set check + `isIpcError` now verifies the kind is in the known set; adding an IpcError variant in `lib/types.ts` produces a compile error at the array literal via the satisfies constraint — coupling type definition + runtime guard, type-driven instead of two-places-to-update. (P2 code-reviewer lazy double-import) `lazy` was imported in a second `from "solid-js"` statement separate from the main destructured import; would fail `no-duplicate-imports` lint under stricter ESLint configs. Merged into the main import block. (P3 silent-failure preview-mode invisibility) browser-preview mock's only "tell" was `blake3:00...00` in the small commentary aside footer; a developer running `pnpm dev` in a browser tab who forgets `isTauri()` is false could think they exercised the real IPC path. Added a yellow `role="status"` banner gated on `!isTauri()` reading "Preview mode (no Tauri). Play returns a mock result, not a real sim run." Visible above the header as the first thing the eye lands on. **Deferred to follow-up rows (commit body):** type-design P2 (`Minute` branded type for tick→minute conversion — defer to T1-9 when client-side conversion lands; today the sim provides `event.minute` directly so there's no client-side conversion to type-guard); type-design P3 (dev-board toggle struct consolidation `createSignal<{visible: boolean; renderedFor: {seedHex, ticks} | null}>` for compile-time invariant "dev-board can only be visible if there's a last-run to render it from" — defer to T4-1 when real dot rendering + prop-driven `FrameDrivenBoard` extraction lands); silent-failure P2 (dev-board rendering wrong seed substantively — defer to T4-1 same as type-design P3; T1-6 fix-pass added the warning label so the misalignment is surfaced not silent, the structural fix requires the prop-driven board extraction). **Cross-crate scope expansion authorized**: 4 new frontend devDeps + new vitest.config.ts + test-setup.ts (substrate-warming for T1-13's broader Vitest gate). Match.tsx 109 → ~590 LoC. 271 LoC Match.test.tsx + 33 LoC vitest.config.ts + 8 LoC test-setup.ts. 10 new tests. 9 files changed end-to-end (Match.tsx rewrite + 3 new files + lib/types.ts MatchEvent.kind tightening + package.json devDeps + pnpm-lock.yaml + MASTER_PLAN + MEMORY + STATUS + CHANGELOG). `scripts/fw verify` clean post fix-pass (cargo fmt + clippy + workspace tests + canonical-hash regression on `782fcde6…8c0f` + banned-terms + determinism-audit + fw-content-baker validate); `pnpm typecheck` + `pnpm lint` + `pnpm test` (10/10) + `pnpm build` (Match chunk 10.11 kB gzipped 3.73 kB) all clean. **The match-engine vertical is now complete end-to-end from sim through user-visible frontend.** T1-3.5 makes ball actions happen → T1-4a emits MatchEvents → T1-4b renders Tracery commentary → T1-11 wires signatures into normal tick_match → T1-12 hardens content validation → T1-5 ships the typed IPC contract → T1-6 surfaces the user-visible Match page. **T1-10 is next** per audit-triage order (4th of 5 audit P1s — `fw-core` SIGMOID_LUT + EXP_LUT bake-time replacement of runtime f64 generation; cross-OS hash currently passes but libm/platform-dependent by design). After T1-10: T1-13 (frontend test gate + cargo audit) is the 5th + final audit-triage P1. (this commit)
- 2026-05-16 — **T1-12 Content validation hardening (Codex 2026-05-16 audit P1 closure — three-prong: duplicate-ID fail-loud + unwired-validators fail-loud + serde-bypasses-try_new fail-loud).** Closes the 3rd of 4 Codex 2026-05-16 audit P1 findings (T1-11 + T1-5 closed the other 2 + T1-3.5 closed the P0; remaining: T1-10 LUT bake + T1-13 frontend gate). The hardening prevents 3 distinct silent-failure classes that would have rotted as content authoring scaled: (1) two RON files claiming the same stable ID silently overwriting each other based on `walk_ron_files` lexicographic order → load-order-dependent canonical state with no error surface; (2) bake-time content validators returning `Ok(())` placeholders → future T2-3 wire-up consuming "validated" prompts that were never actually validated; (3) ID newtypes (`RoleId`, `SignatureId`, `SignatureCandidate`) with `try_new` validators that derived-Deserialize bypassed → fixture files with malformed IDs passing load with the invalid value silently propagated. gameplay-programmer shipped 6 chunks: (1) New `ContentLoadError::DuplicateId { kind: &'static str, id: String, path_first: PathBuf, path_dupe: PathBuf }` variant in `fw-content::runtime` + `fn insert_unique<V>(map: &mut BTreeMap<String, V>, paths_seen: &mut BTreeMap<String, PathBuf>, id: String, value: V, kind: &'static str, path: PathBuf) -> Result<(), ContentLoadError>` helper. Helper checks `paths_seen.get(&id)` BEFORE mutating the map (preserves first value on error path); on collision returns `DuplicateId` with both paths for fail-loud reporting. 3 unit tests on insert_unique directly. (2) Refactored all 5 `BTreeMap::insert` sites in `ContentStore::load_sources` (cultures / archetypes / role-affinities / players / signatures) to go through `insert_unique`. Each loader-block tracks its own local `paths_seen` (no cross-category leakage since IDs across kinds are independent). kind strings consistent snake_case: `"culture"` / `"archetype"` / `"role_affinity_table"` / `"player_template"` / `"signature_definition"`. (3) Integration test `crates/fw-content/tests/duplicate_id_test.rs` (NEW) — 5 tests (one per kind) writing 2 RON fixtures with the same stable ID into a tmpdir + asserting `DuplicateId{kind, id, path_first, path_dupe}` with matching fields. Test fixtures use minimal valid RON (just the id field for each kind; other required fields zero-valued where Q32 invariants permit). (4) New `ValidationError::NotImplemented { validator: &'static str, defer_to: &'static str }` variant in `fw-content-baker::validators`. 3 check_ functions (`check_banned_terms` / `check_licensed_data` / `check_cliche`) switched from `Ok(())` stubs to returning `NotImplemented` with actionable defer_to strings: `"T2-3"` for banned_terms (bake-names pipeline first consumer per the row-text deferral target); `"T3+"` for licensed_data + cliche (LLM bake review). `validate_fragment` short-circuits via `?` so callers see `banned_terms` first. 4 unit tests pin variant fields + ordering. `run_validate` doc-comment at `fw-content-baker/src/main.rs:219-226` updated to reflect the T1-12 change: validators now fail-closed via NotImplemented; the `validate` subcommand doesn't invoke them so `cargo run -p fw-content-baker -- validate` continues passing as expected until T2-3 wires the bake-time validators through the bake pipeline (confirmed via `scripts/fw verify`). (5) Manual `impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for RoleId` in `fw-content::role_affinity` calling `String::deserialize(d)?` then `RoleId::try_new(s).map_err(serde::de::Error::custom)`. Removed `#[derive(Deserialize)]` + `#[serde(transparent)]` from RoleId; kept `#[derive(Serialize)]` + `#[serde(transparent)]` so the wire form is unchanged (RoleId still serialises as a bare RON string). (6) Manual `Deserialize` for `SignatureId` via Visitor implementing `visit_str` + `visit_string` + `visit_newtype_struct` — all 3 paths terminate in `try_new` (no fall-through; no path accepts arbitrary strings). Visitor approach chosen over `#[serde(try_from = "String")]` to handle the three RON wire forms (`SignatureId("...")` newtype-struct form, bare-string form, and the visit_string fallback) symmetrically — type-design audit noted this is defensible for an ID type a mod author may construct in either form. Manual `Deserialize` for `SignatureCandidate` via private `RawSignatureCandidate { signature_id, affinity }` + `TryFrom` bridge running `try_new(signature_id, affinity)`. Integration test `crates/fw-content/tests/serde_validation_test.rs` (NEW) — 12 tests across all 3 types (4 each): valid form parses cleanly + 3 boundary-fail variations per type (RoleId: empty / whitespace-only / leading-whitespace; SignatureId: malformed / wrong-entity-type / single-segment-pack; SignatureCandidate: malformed-sig-id / affinity-above-one / affinity-negative). Each test asserts error message substance (`msg.contains("malformed")` etc.), not just "an Err is returned" — non-vacuous per the iii-c lesson. 24 new tests total. **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `782fcde6…8c0f`** — T1-12 touches loader-side validation only; canonical match-state bytes are unaffected. `cargo run -p fw-content-baker -- validate` continues passing (acceptance #7 confirmed — the validate subcommand structural validators run; the bake-time validators it doesn't invoke now return NotImplemented). **Self-review triple verdicts:** silent-failure-hunter ACCEPT (2 P3 nits, both deferred — auditor's `id.clone()` concern was a misread on inspection [the existing code already has the minimum-1 clone the BTreeMap-pair requires]; commentary build-error flattening to `MissingCommentaryGrammar` is a knowingly-accepted shortcut already documented inline at runtime.rs:136-137 with explicit deferral note). type-design-analyzer ACCEPT (2 P2 + 2 P3 — `From<&str>`/`From<String>` panic-backdoor on RoleId [FIX], `&'static str` kind label vs LoaderKind enum [analyst recommended accept-as-is at T1-12 + revisit at T2-3 mod-overlay loading], SignatureId Visitor over-engineered vs `try_from = "String"` [analyst recommended accept — Visitor handles 3 wire forms symmetrically], RawSignatureCandidate correctly private). code-reviewer REVISE (1 P1 + 1 P2 — tmpdir no-cleanup + pid-only-uniqueness CI-retry-collision-prone; vacuous `path_first.exists() || !path_first.as_os_str().is_empty()` assertion that short-circuited on always-true second arm so existence was never actually checked). **Second clean silent-failure-hunter verdict in a row** (T1-11 was the first; T1-5 had 1 P1 + 3 P2/P3) — structural anti-failure work is compounding. Main-thread fix-pass per cargo-cult-fix-pass meta-pattern (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15) closed all P1/P2 in-place: (P1 code-reviewer tmpdir) added `tempfile = "3"` to workspace `[workspace.dependencies]` + `fw-content` `[dev-dependencies]`; rewrote all 5 duplicate_id_test functions from `make_tmpdir(pid, suffix)` to `TempDir::new()` for auto-cleanup + random per-directory suffix. The collision class this closes: under parallel `cargo test` invocations (CI matrix shares pid across binary tests within the same process, AND a developer running two terminal tabs concurrently OR CI retry on the same job) the static `"fwh-dup-{pid}-{suffix}"` directory was reused without cleanup → leftover `.ron` files from prior runs would silently corrupt the next run's assertion via stale third-file presence. `TempDir` solves this with random per-call directory naming + automatic cleanup on drop. (P2 code-reviewer vacuous-assert) Original assertion `path_first.exists() || !path_first.as_os_str().is_empty()` was unfalsifiable — `path_first` is always derived from a non-empty tmpdir path so the second operand evaluates to `true` unconditionally, short-circuiting the `||` and never reaching `exists()`. Dropped the `||` guard; assert `path_first.exists()` + `path_dupe.exists()` directly with diagnostic messages including the actual paths. The assertion now catches the failure mode that matters: the DuplicateId variant stored real on-disk paths, not e.g. `PathBuf::from("")`. (P2 type-design F P2-1) `impl From<&str> for RoleId` + `impl From<String> for RoleId` removed entirely. - 2026-05-16 — **T1-11 Wire signatures into real match setup (Codex 2026-05-16 audit P1 closure).** Closes Codex audit P1 "Signatures are implemented but unreachable in normal tick_match." T1-2b-iv built the signature dispatcher + tests fired signatures via custom fixtures, but the normal `tick_match` path passed `&BTreeMap::new()` for sig_definitions + initial players had empty signature_candidates → real matches NEVER fired a signature. This row wires the dispatcher into the normal path end-to-end so the dev-board scrubber + Tauri IPC (`play_match`/`match_frames`) + T1-4b's commentary renderer all see real signature firings. The match-engine inner loop is now narrating real football: T1-3.5 makes ball actions happen → T1-4a emits MatchEvents into canonical state → T1-4b renders Tracery commentary → T1-11 makes signatures actually fire in the normal path. gameplay-programmer shipped 6 chunks: (1) folded in Codex T1-4b P2 type-design — added `MatchEvent::discriminant(&self) -> u8` method (initially returned u8; fix-pass below changed to MatchEventDiscriminant typed enum) in `fw-content::event` as single source of truth, refactored `MatchEventDiscriminant::from_event` + canonical encoder's `write_u8(0..5)` literals to consume it, new cross-crate `event_discriminant_test.rs` (242 LoC, 8 tests) pinning the byte values against a hardcoded table that catches any future variant-reordering regression. (2) New `MatchState::initial_with_content(seed: Seed, content: &ContentStore) -> Result<MatchState, ContentInitError>` constructor variant in `fw-match-sim::lib` — builds the 22-player baseline via existing `MatchState::initial(seed)`, then projects slot 7's (home AM) `signature_candidates` from a `preferred_role.as_str() == "AM"` PlayerTemplate (agent semantically deviated from spec's literal `"sample-am"` file-stem lookup since `ContentStore.player_templates` is keyed by `qualified_id` not stem; the semantic role-based lookup is the right call). Other 21 slots keep empty signature_candidates (T1-7 procgen fills the rest when real per-template player population lands). Fail-loud per T1-12 hardening pattern: new `ContentInitError::MissingTemplate { key: String }` variant with helpful Display message; no silent fallback to empty candidates. 6 unit tests cover deterministic same-seed identity + slot-7 projection + slot-other invariance + fails_when_sample_am_missing for the empty-store case. (3) `tick_match` signature changed from `tick_match(state: MatchState) -> MatchState` to `tick_match(state: MatchState, sig_definitions: &BTreeMap<String, SignatureDefinition>) -> MatchState` — backwards-incompatible API change; 27 call sites updated across fw-match-sim tests + fw-tauri commands.rs (all pass `&BTreeMap::new()` to preserve existing no-signatures behavior unless they explicitly need content). (4) `dump_frames --content <PATH>` clap flag; when present, uses `ContentStore::load_sources(<path>)` + `MatchState::initial_with_content(seed, &store)` + threads `&store.signature_definitions` to each `tick_match` call; when absent, falls back to existing `MatchState::initial` + empty sig_definitions (preserves the existing dev-board fixture pipeline). New `signature_wiring_smoke_test.rs` (83 LoC) — runs 600-tick `initial_with_content` smoke flow + asserts ≥1 `MatchEvent::SignatureFirstFired` for slot 7 (non-vacuous per the T1-3.5/T1-4b lesson: assert candidate_ids non-empty FIRST, then ≥1 firing, then every fired_id is in the candidate list). (5) Workspace verification clean. (6) **Canonical hash NOT rebaselined** — the smoke-seed regression test uses `MatchState::initial(seed)` + `&BTreeMap::new()` for sig_definitions, NOT the new `initial_with_content` path; T1-11 adds a parallel constructor without disturbing the existing path. Hash stays at `782fcde6…8c0f`. **Self-review triple** landed: silent-failure-hunter ACCEPT (no P0/P1 — first clean verdict in the T1-2b → T1-11 saga; the structural anti-failure work compounded), type-design-analyzer ACCEPT with 1 P1 (discriminant return type), code-reviewer REVISE with 1 P0 + 1 P1. Main-thread fix-pass per the cargo-cult meta-pattern (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15) closed all 3: (P0 code-reviewer Critical) `fw-tauri::commands.rs` `play_match` + `match_frames` STILL passed `&std::collections::BTreeMap::new()` to `tick_match` — the agent wired `dump_frames` correctly but missed the Tauri IPC path which is the production game route. Silent-failure-hunter missed this because the prompt focused on renderer + constructor not cross-crate IPC; code-reviewer caught it via cross-file diff scan. Fix: added `load_content_for_command()` helper using `FW_CONTENT_PATH` env-var override (defaults to `"content"` for `pnpm tauri dev` from project root); both `play_match` + `match_frames` now load ContentStore + use `initial_with_content` + thread `&content.signature_definitions` through `tick_match`. T1-5 follow-up: lift to AppState-cached ContentStore (constructed at app startup, threaded through every command via `&AppState`) — avoids the per-command load cost + supports Tauri's resource resolver for bundled-app paths (T5-1 Steam distribution). fw-tauri integration tests updated to set `FW_CONTENT_PATH` to workspace-root content via `CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR`-relative path (Rust 2024 unsafe block on env::set_var per the new thread-safety lint). (P1 type-design) `MatchEvent::discriminant()` return type changed `u8` → `MatchEventDiscriminant` typed enum; `MatchEventDiscriminant` moved from `fw-content::commentary` to `fw-content::event` (cyclic-import otherwise since event.rs now consumes the type); `MatchEventDiscriminant::from_event` simplified to trivial passthrough `event.discriminant()` (was byte→enum→byte round-trip with `unreachable!()` panic landmine on unknown bytes — the typed return removes both); canonical encoder writes `self.write_u8(event.discriminant() as u8)` (one cast at point of use; sound via `#[repr(u8)]` on the enum); commentary.rs has `pub use crate::event::MatchEventDiscriminant;` for backwards compat with existing downstream consumers; `event_discriminant_test.rs` callsites updated to `.discriminant() as u8` for byte assertions. (P1 code-reviewer) `signature_wiring_smoke_test.rs` event-scan was O(n²) — scanned the full `match_events()` Vec on every tick → events from tick T got re-counted on ticks T+1..600. The `!is_empty()` assertion still passed correctly (one real firing is enough), but the accumulator's count + per-id iteration were silently measuring the wrong thing. Fix: track `prev_event_count` and slice from there — only NEW events appended per tick get scanned. T1-5's row body should be amended to absorb the AppState ContentStore migration the T1-11 fix-pass deferred to it. (this commit)

(P3 fix-pass bonus, code-reviewer) temp-dir race fix: replaced shared `/tmp/fwh-test-no-commentary` literal with `PID + thread::id()` suffix — NOT SystemTime (that's banned by Sim/RULES.md §3 determinism-audit; caught when audit flagged my initial SystemTime fix). **Default impl follow-up**: tightening `try_from_map` broke `ContentStore::default()` which constructed bank with empty-variant origins; updated the Default impl to use non-empty placeholder strings `format!("(default placeholder for {disc:?})")` so the test-only constructor still satisfies the new invariant — same placeholder mechanism, now correctly typed. P2/P3 deferred to follow-up rows (captured in commit body): `MissingGrammarError` collecting all missing (only reports first); `walk_files_with_ext` silently skips unknown stems (no log on `other` arm); missing-dir reports as `MissingCommentaryGrammar { KickOff }` (misleading specific-error for a directory-level absence — needs dedicated `MissingDir` variant); three-way discriminant duplication between `MatchEvent` variants + `MatchEventDiscriminant` + canonical encoder `write_u8` literals (recommend `MatchEvent::discriminant()` method as single source — touches fw-match-sim::canonical which is out-of-scope for T1-4b; defer to dedicated cleanup row); `tracery::Error` leaks into ContentLoadError public surface (cross-crate API hygiene; defer); per-render Grammar rebuild cost (T2 perf flag when event volume scales); `#onTarget#` unused in shot.tracery.json (Tracery no-conditionals limitation; T2 pre-computes derived `#shotResult#`); DECISIONS factual error claiming "0x10..0x17 contiguous" (actual layers are 0x10..0x14, 0x20, 0x30, 0x40 — Commentary at 0x18 sits in the 0x15..0x1F gap; needs append-only supersede entry to correct; defer to a `/log-decision` cycle). **Cross-crate scope expansions authorized**: workspace deps `tracery 0.2.1` + `rand 0.8` (user nod at row-spec time; rand 0.8 brought in transitively + already used in fw-content::runtime). +221/-15 modified + 873 LoC new code (637 commentary.rs + 236 integration test); 30 new tracery template variants in 6 files (content); 18 new tests passing (14 in commentary.rs + 4 in integration test). **Canonical hash UNCHANGED** through both substrate + fix-pass (templates are content data; renderer output is read-side projection of match_events into prose; not part of canonical MatchState). `scripts/fw verify` clean post fix-pass. **T1-4 row fully closed** across T1-4a (sim emission, commit 5b3b8bd) + T1-4b (commentary, this commit). **T1-11 is next** per audit-triage order — signature wiring into normal `tick_match` (currently passes `BTreeMap::new()` for sig_definitions; signatures only fire in custom test fixtures; T1-11 wires real content-store-projected definitions + per-player `signature_candidates` so commentary has signatures to narrate). - 2026-05-16 — **T1-3.5 ball mutation + possession state + goal detection (Codex 2026-05-16 audit P0 closure; gates T1-4b).** Closes Codex 2026-05-16 whole-codebase audit's P0 finding — the fundamental problem that T1-4a's `MatchEvent` emission described player intentions but the underlying sim didn't actually do football (Shot intent set player vel toward target but didn't mutate ball.vel; Pass intent did nothing to the ball; no possession transfer; no goal detection). This row makes football actually happen end-to-end on the normal match path. **New canonical-state fields**: `possession: Option<PlayerSlot>` + `last_touched_by: Option<PlayerSlot>` on `MatchState`, both `pub(crate)` with `possession()` + `last_touched_by()` public accessors per the T1-4a P1 pattern; `with_last_touched_by` + `with_match_end_tick` builders for test scaffolding. Initial state: `Some(9)` (home centre forward) for both. **Pitch geometry constants** (`fw-core::lib`): `PITCH_LENGTH_M = 105m`, `PITCH_WIDTH_M = 68m` (FIFA standard); `GOAL_LINE_X` + `SIDELINE_Y` const-derived via raw-bit shift (`Q32::from_raw(PITCH_LENGTH_M.to_bits() >> 1)`) for single-source-of-truth — same family as T1-4a's `GOAL_HALF_WIDTH_M` lesson. **Ball-speed constants + helpers** (`fw-match-sim::dispatch`): `SHOT_BASE_SPEED_MPS = 20` + `SHOT_PEAK_BONUS_MPS = 15` scaled by `strength × finishing` (range 20-35 m/s); `PASS_BASE_SPEED_MPS = 15` + `PASS_PEAK_BONUS_MPS = 10` scaled by `passing × vision` (range 15-25 m/s). `compute_ball_speed_for_shot/pass` pure helpers + `ball_unit_vel(from_x, from_y, to_x, to_y, speed) -> (Q32, Q32)` using cordic-Q32 sqrt (no f64); zero-distance fallback returns `(Q32::ZERO, Q32::ZERO)` (post-fix-pass; prior `(speed, Q32::ZERO)` +X-at-full-speed could fire a phantom goal if a clustered passer near positive goal line had from==to receiver). **Ball mutation in `apply_intent`**: AttemptShot computes shot-speed via helper + sets `ball.vel` toward target via `ball_unit_vel` + transfers `possession = None` + sets `last_touched_by = Some(shooter_slot)`. AttemptPassShort/AttemptPassLong/Cross/LayOff compute pass-speed + set ball.vel toward receiver's position + transfer `possession = Some(to_slot)` + `last_touched_by = Some(from_slot)`. Dribble updates `last_touched_by` + snaps `ball.pos` to player position. GkDistributeShort/GkDistributeLong mirror Pass. Exhaustive PlayerIntent match preserves T1-4a P0-1 wildcard-removal discipline. **`tick_match` step ordering**: new step 2 (goal detection BEFORE physics) + new step 3 (OOB clamp BEFORE physics, gated by `goal_fired_this_tick` flag added in fix-pass for the post-goal-reset double-clamp guard); old step 7 (FullTime) moved to step 9. Step 2 goal detection: if `|ball.pos_x| >= GOAL_LINE_X && |ball.pos_y| < GOAL_HALF_WIDTH_M` → emit `MatchEvent::Goal` + bump home_score (when bx_bits > 0, away conceded) or away_score (when bx_bits < 0, home conceded) via `checked_add + expect` (fix-pass replaced `saturating_add` per Codex Q1 panic-on-overflow policy) + reset ball to centre_spot + emit `MatchEvent::KickOff { is_second_half: false }` + derive kick-off taker from who conceded (home concedes → slot 9; away concedes → slot 20 = slot 11 + 9 offset) and set possession + last_touched_by accordingly (fix-pass closure for Codex code-reviewer Critical #1 — prior `Some(9)` unconditional misrouted possession after away goals) + call `tactic_fsm::apply_event(..., TacticEvent::Goal, ...)` on BOTH teams' tactic states (closes the Codex 2026-05-16 audit backlog item "tactic FSM event transitions are not integrated" — for Goal only; other variants stay unintegrated). Step 3 OOB clamp: if past sideline OR past non-goal goal-line → zero ball.vel_x/vel_y (vel_z preserved per fix-pass doc note — altitude motion is orthogonal to pitch-plane OOB) + clamp ball.pos to nearest boundary; emit nothing (throw-in/corner/goal-kick = Phase 2). The step-ordering reversal (vs spec's step 7+8 AFTER physics) was driven by an underlying coord-convention bug discovered during implementation: `ball_physics.rs` was treating `pos_y` as altitude (gravity on -Y), contradicting the 2D pitch convention used everywhere else in `fw-match-sim`. **Substrate fix authorized**: ball_physics now uses `pos_z` as altitude (gravity on -Z; rolling friction acts on pitch-plane XY); 6 ball-physics tests updated; new altitude-axis regression test (`gravity_acts_on_altitude_axis_z_not_lateral_axis_y`) pins the corrected convention so a future regression to "Y = altitude" fails loudly. Doc-comment fixes per fix-pass: `ball.rs` lines 13-17 rewritten (was citing old X+Z=pitch convention); `lib.rs` OOB rationale rewritten (was citing now-obsolete "physics would heal a negative lateral pos"); `ball_physics.rs` step-8 reference corrected to step-3 with inverted temporal framing. **Encoder VERSION 7→8** with new `match_end_tick i64 LE` (T1-4a) + new possession/last_touched_by section after match_events (Option<u8> = 1-byte tag + 1-byte value). **Canonical hash REBASELINED** per ADR-0012 trigger #1: `02ab97d0…27e686` → `782fcde6…8c0f`. 3 new test files: `ball_mutation_proptest.rs` (3 proptest invariants — determinism via canonical encoding + shot.vel non-zero + pass.vel non-zero, all anti-vacuous per T1-2b-fix meta-pattern), `goal_detection_unit_tests.rs` (401 LoC, covers Goal/OOB/tactic-FSM-hookup with direct dispatch-path tests not in-memory bypass), altitude-axis regression test added to ball_physics.rs. **Self-review triple landed Revise across all 3 agents** (silent-failure-hunter / type-design-analyzer / code-reviewer) with **7 P0 + 6 P1 findings** heavily overlapping. All P0s + most P1s closed in main-thread fix-pass per the cargo-cult meta-pattern (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15): (P0-1) `unwrap_or(0)` scorer attribution at lib.rs:500 — replaced with `.expect()` carrying binding invariant (same family as T1-4a deleted `apply_tactic_event_with_emission` helper); (P0-2) `ball.rs` stale coord-convention doc-comment rewritten; (P0-3) `lib.rs` OOB-clamp rationale rewritten post coord-fix; (P0-4) `ball_physics.rs` stale "step 8" reference corrected; (P0-5) OOB clamp `goal_fired_this_tick` guard added; (P0-6) kick-off taker derived from who conceded; (P0-7) `ball_unit_vel` zero-distance returns ZERO not +X-speed; (P1-1) `checked_add` replaces `saturating_add` on score bump; (P1-2) `with_match_end_tick` test builder added; (P1-3) vel_z OOB carve-out documented; (P1-type-design) GOAL_LINE_X / SIDELINE_Y const-derived from parents via raw-bit shift; (P1-altitude-regression-test) added. P2/P3 deferred to follow-up rows (in commit body): Option<PlayerSlot> → PossessionState enum; last_touched_by ghost-touch model; with_last_touched_by visibility; compute_ball_speed_* migration to PlayerState methods; pitch-size-from-content-pack; nearest_teammate_near debug_assert → assert; Dribble doesn't reset ball.pos_z; Shot emission doc for T2 contest model; wide-of-posts test missing OOB assertions; feature-gating default_zero + max_baseline test helpers (would require workspace feature plumbing — deferred). **Cross-crate scope expansions authorized**: pitch geometry constants → fw-core (substrate); `PlayerAttributes::default_zero` test helper added to fw-core (kept pub for now with "test-only" doc-comment; feature-gating deferred). +1190 / -155 code lines (after fix-pass). `scripts/fw verify` clean post fix-pass; canonical hash unchanged through fix-pass (smoke seed at 60 ticks doesn't reach the kick-off-taker or zero-distance code paths). **T1-4b unblocked** — narrative-director-owned Tracery commentary templates can now narrate real football outcomes, not player-intent placeholders. (this commit)
- 2026-05-16 — **T1-4a MatchEvent emission + canonical encoding (sim side; T1-4 row split half-1 of 2).** Implements ADR-0007 §"Layer 1 — Diagnostic commentary" sim-side scaffolding + ADR-0005 §"Event class catalogue" Phase-1 subset. New `crates/fw-content/src/event.rs` (NEW, 351 LoC) ships `MatchEvent` enum with 6 variants: `KickOff { tick, is_second_half }`, `FullTime { tick, home_score, away_score }`, `Goal { scorer_slot, tick, score_home_after, score_away_after }`, `Shot { shooter_slot, tick, target_x, target_y, on_target }`, `Pass { from_slot, to_slot, tick, kind, completed }` with `PassKind { Short, Long, Cross, LayOff }` sub-enum, `SignatureFirstFired { player_slot, signature_id, tick }`. Plus `GOAL_HALF_WIDTH_M: Q32 = 15_720_299_520_i64` (3.66 m half-width single-source-of-truth) + `is_shot_on_target(target_y)` helper using `unsigned_abs()` (no `i64::MIN.abs()` panic). 10 unit tests on the new types (6 serde round-trips + PassKind distinctness + on_target bounds + size probe). `PlayerSlot = u8` moved from `fw-match-sim::lib` to `fw-core::lib` (small substrate cleanup; same pattern as T1-2b-fix's SeedLayer move; required so `fw-content::event::MatchEvent` can reference PlayerSlot without creating a dep cycle since fw-content can't depend on fw-match-sim). `MatchState` gained `match_events: Vec<MatchEvent>` (canonical state; NOT serde-skip) + `match_end_tick: Tick` (T1: hardcoded `Tick::from_raw(60)` for smoke-seed budget; T1-5 will add a constructor variant for configurable end ticks). Both fields are `pub(crate)` with `MatchState::match_events() -> &[MatchEvent]` + `MatchState::match_end_tick() -> Tick` public accessors per the `signature_candidates` visibility pattern from T1-2b-iv P1-2. `signature_memory_events: Vec<MemoryEvent>` field REMOVED from MatchState (its semantic — append-only ledger-eligible event stream — subsumed by `match_events`); `crates/fw-match-sim/src/signature/ledger.rs` DELETED (82 LoC; the local `MemoryEvent::SignatureFirstFired` stub now lives as `MatchEvent::SignatureFirstFired` in fw-content). Canonical encoder VERSION 6→7 with new `match_end_tick i64 LE` + length-prefixed `match_events` section after `signature_first_fired_seen`. Per-event encoding: discriminant u8 + variant-specific fields in stable byte order (KickOff=0, FullTime=1, Goal=2, Shot=3, Pass=4, SignatureFirstFired=5; do NOT reorder — canonical-hash-pinned). 5 live emission paths wired: KickOff at `MatchState::initial` (first event ever, tick=0), FullTime at `tick_match` step 7 with `state.tick >= match_end_tick && !already_emitted` guard (fix-pass changed from `==` to `>=` after silent-failure-hunter caught the brittleness — caller over-advancing past `match_end_tick` would silently lose FullTime emission), Shot from `apply_intent` when intent is `PlayerIntent::AttemptShot`, Pass from `apply_intent` for `AttemptPassShort`/`AttemptPassLong`/`Cross`/`LayOff` (4 sites all using a `T1_PASS_COMPLETED: bool = true` const so T2's contest model wires a single grep-anchored change), SignatureFirstFired in `dispatch_tick` (reconciled from the deleted signature::ledger stub; same trigger semantics — first-fire-per-(slot, sig)-pair via `signature_first_fired_seen.insert`). **`MatchEvent::Goal` ships forward-compat-only**: enum variant exists + canonical encoder arm at discriminant 2 + new `encode_match_event_goal_arm_is_exercised` unit test in canonical.rs covers the encoder path even without live emission. The `apply_tactic_event_with_emission` helper that would have emitted Goal was DELETED in the fix-pass (its `scorer_slot.unwrap_or(0)` fallback would have silently misattributed unattributed goals to slot 0 = the home goalkeeper). Real Goal emission waits on T1-9/T2 ball-in-net detection + possession-chain scorer attribution (the call-site protocol is documented in `fw_match_sim::lib` as a deletion-note comment). 3 new test files: `crates/fw-match-sim/tests/match_event_snapshot.rs` (insta snapshot of 60-tick event stream with 3 anti-vacuousness guards — `!is_empty()`, first event is KickOff, last is FullTime — before the snapshot fires; the snapshot would otherwise be vacuously satisfied even on a broken emission path), `crates/fw-match-sim/tests/match_event_proptest.rs` (2 proptests — `events_chronological` asserts tick monotonic non-decreasing across 100 random seeds × 60 ticks with `len >= 2` positive-emission guard; `determinism_across_runs` asserts same seed run twice produces byte-identical match_events Vec via `encode_canonical()` equality + per-event direct comparison), `signature_dispatcher_proptest.rs AC-5` migrated from reading the deleted `signature_memory_events` to reading `match_events()` (same observable semantics, same exactly-1-per-(slot,sig) invariant). Test count delta: +13 unit + 2 proptest + 1 snapshot = 16 new tests; 1 test migrated (AC-5). **Canonical hash REBASELINED per ADR-0012 trigger #1**: `blake3:d376ba26…fa93` → `blake3:02ab97d0…27e686`. Atomic update to `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs::PINNED_60_TICK` + `crates/fw-replay/fixtures/0xdeadbeefdeadbeef.ron::expected_hash` + `crates/fw-content/tests/fixtures_load.rs::EXPECTED` (3 pinned locations now agree). Encoder VERSION progression: 1 (T0/T1-2b-i) → 2 (T1-2b-ii) → 3 (T1-2b-iii-a) → 4 (T1-2b-iii-b) → 5 (T1-2b-iv) → 6 (T1-2b-fix round 1; signature_candidates encoding + cross-category stacking; not previously called out in MEMORY) → 7 (T1-4a; match_events + match_end_tick + signature_memory_events removal). **Self-review triple landed Revise across all 3 agents** (silent-failure-hunter / type-design-analyzer / code-reviewer) with 8 P0/P1 findings overlapping heavily across the three reports. Closed in a main-thread fix-pass per the cargo-cult-fix-pass meta-pattern (DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15 — "by round 3 on the same finding family, switch authorship"; main-threaded the fix even on round 1 here because the round-3-on-same-finding-family rule has been generalized to "main-thread fixes touching substance, not just shape"): (1) `GOAL_HALF_WIDTH_M` const + `is_shot_on_target` literal disagreement (~20mm drift; const was computed wrong via `3_i64 << 32 | bits` AND wasn't referenced from the function which used inline literal) — single source of truth now (const set to function's value; function reads const). (2) `_ => {}` catch-all in apply_intent emission match would silently drop events for any future pass-like PlayerIntent variant (P0 silent-failure that would land undetected; same family as the T1-2b-iv `intent_to_bias_consideration` wildcard removal lesson per T1-2b-iv P0-3 fix-pass) — exhaustive enumeration of all 14 non-emitting variants forces compile-error on future additions. (3) FullTime `==` check would never fire if caller over-advanced — switched to `>= && !already_emitted` with O(1) last-event-inspection guard pinning the "FullTime at most once" invariant. (4) `match_events`/`match_end_tick` `pub` → `pub(crate)` + accessors per the signature_candidates P1-2 pattern (the `pub` fields invited `state.match_events.clear()` / `state.match_end_tick = Tick::ZERO` from external code; pub(crate) makes that impossible). (5) `is_shot_on_target` panicked on `i64::MIN.abs()` (undefined behavior path) — switched to `target_y.to_bits().unsigned_abs()` which returns u64. (6) `nearest_teammate_near` had the same `i64::MIN.abs()` panic risk on distance computation + silent self-pass fallback when `best_slot = passer_slot` was never overwritten — i128 cast for distance arithmetic (Q32 raw bits fit comfortably; no overflow) + `debug_assert_ne!(best_slot, passer_slot)` after the loop pinning "loop must always find a teammate" invariant. (7) `Pass.completed: true` hardcoded at 4 sites without inline pointer to T2 contest model — extracted `T1_PASS_COMPLETED: bool = true` const with grep-anchoring doc-comment so T2 wiring surfaces every call site via a single rename. (8) `MatchEvent::Goal` structurally unreachable via the dead `apply_tactic_event_with_emission` helper with `scorer_slot.unwrap_or(0)` silent slot-0-misattribution — DELETED the helper, kept variant + encoder + new direct unit test as forward-compat scaffolding. P2/P3 deferred to T1-9/T2/separate-row work: `PlayerSlot` newtype migration (worthwhile but ~30-50 callsites; defer); `Shot.on_target` redundancy with `target_y` (cache-vs-compute trade-off; defer); `Shot.{target_x, target_y}` bounds enforcement via try_new validator (low risk today; defer); `PassKind` #[non_exhaustive] (cheap insurance but no immediate need; defer); `events_chronological` proptest not exercising Pass/Shot paths because BTreeMap::new() is passed to dispatch (skeleton-tier BT doesn't naturally produce those intents on smoke seed; defer to T1-8 replay corpus when more events fire); cosmetic borrow-checker rationale comment in apply_intent; `prop_assume!` in events_chronological deleted as dead code (KickOff is unconditionally pushed; assume never triggers; `assert!(len >= 2)` is the real gate). Cross-crate scope expansions authorized: PlayerSlot → fw-core (substrate cleanup); MatchEvent → fw-content::event (cross-crate type for T1-4b consumption); MatchState 2 new pub(crate) fields. **Cross-crate dep adjustments**: `fw-content/Cargo.toml` gained `serde_json` as `[dev-dependencies]` for MatchEvent round-trip tests. ~638/-168 code lines after fix-pass; 1 new source file (event.rs, 351 LoC); 2 new test files (snapshot + proptest); 1 file deleted (signature/ledger.rs). `scripts/fw verify` green end-to-end post fix-pass. **T1-4b queued behind a `/log-decision` prereq**: ADR-0009 needs a `SeedLayer::Commentary` discriminant for T1-4b's deterministic Tracery variant-pick; without that ADR amendment, T1-4b has no canonical seed layer to derive its variant-selection RNG seed from. (this commit)
- 2026-05-16 — **T1-2b-fix round 4 — AC-2 cooldown-observable rewrite (final Codex P1-8 gap).** Codex Tier-2 round-3 re-audit returned REVISE with one narrow remaining issue: AC-2 in `signature_dispatcher_proptest.rs` was using `signature_first_fired_seen.iter().filter(...).count()` as the observable to verify cooldown enforcement. That field is `BTreeSet<(PlayerSlot, SignatureId)>` — set membership by construction cannot have duplicates, so a bad implementation that re-fires every tick inside the 600-tick cooldown window would still pass the assertion (each re-fire's `insert` into the seen-set is a no-op once the entry exists). Codex correctly identified this as "wrong observable for cooldown enforcement" — the test passed for the right reason on a correct impl but would also pass for the wrong reason on a broken impl. Rewrite swaps to two observables that ARE load-bearing under re-fire: (1) `signature_cooldowns[&key]` — a re-fire OVERWRITES this entry via `state.signature_cooldowns.insert((slot, id), cooldown_end_tick)` in `dispatch.rs`, so on every tick of the 500-tick cooldown window the test asserts the captured `cooldown_end_first` value is unchanged; (2) `signature_firing[8][Attacking]` — a re-fire mints a NEW `SignatureFiring { start_tick: T, ... }` with the current tick as `start_tick`, so on every tick the test asserts that if the firing slot is Some, its `start_tick()` equals the captured `first_start_tick` (after the 60-tick active window expires the slot auto-clears to None, and from then until cooldown_end must stay None). Plus a belt-and-suspenders final-state assertion outside the loop. Both observables fail loudly + descriptively on a re-fire bug; the prior set-membership observable did not. Also corrected STATUS.md hash drift: the file still claimed the current pinned hash was `18f1776c…a5d048` (which was T1-2b-iv's rebaseline before T1-2b-fix round 1 rebased it to `d376ba26…fa93` per ADR-0012 trigger #3 for the bias-application restructure). STATUS.md got a substantive rewrite back into state-pointer form per design-docs/RULES.md §6 (was bloated past 150 words with diary entries). MEMORY.md Current task block (~670 lines of the original T1-2b-fix task spec) collapsed to a clean closure pointer; the 4-round saga lives in Recently completed as 4 sequential bullets + the existing DECISIONS.md cargo-cult-pattern entry. Round 4 implementation: ~60 LoC test rewrite + STATUS.md + MEMORY.md hygiene. Main-thread execution per the round-3 mitigation policy ("by round 3 on the same finding family, switch authorship"). `scripts/fw verify` clean. Canonical hash UNCHANGED (test-only behavioral fix). Awaiting Codex round-4 re-audit verdict; round-4 prompt asks specifically to verify the new observables fold on re-fire AND that the STATUS.md hash reads `d376ba26…fa93`. **T1-2b-fix row CLOSED across 4 rounds total**: round 1 (8 P1s + 6 P2s main-thread implementation, `0fc1ae2b`) → round 2 (close-the-gap on P1-5/P2-9 files the agent claimed done but hadn't actually edited, `d6469550`) → round 3 (main-thread fix-pass for recurring fix-shape-without-substance pattern on P1-4 GK bindings + P1-7 cross-category combine + P1-8 AC test rewrites, `ecd41e3`) → round 4 (this commit; observable correctness on AC-2). The 4-round saga is data: the cargo-cult fix-pass pattern survives even strong-AC delegation when the underlying observable is wrong, validating the main-thread-by-round-3 policy from the DECISIONS.md 2026-05-15 entry. (this commit)
- 2026-05-15 — **T1-2b-iv signature dispatcher + first 3 signatures end-to-end. CLOSES T1-2b SUB-PHASE.** Partial implementation of ADR-0011 §"Dispatch + softmax" + §"Cooldown" + §"Stacking policy" + §"Bias snapshot" to validate the dispatcher path end-to-end. New `crates/fw-match-sim/src/signature/` module with 5 submodules: `mod.rs` (`SignatureFiring` carrier with `pub(crate)` fields + `::new()` constructor + `id()` / `start_tick()` / `duration_ticks()` accessors mirroring `TeamTacticState` pattern; `is_active(current_tick)` returns false on retroactive ticks); `triggers.rs` (3 trigger predicate functions — `body_shield_pressure` for defenders within 2m of ball-carrier with marking+strength meeting thresholds, `long_range_strike` for attackers >25m from goal with composure+long_shots meeting thresholds, `first_time_diagonal_switch` for midfielders with vision+passing meeting thresholds AND ball arriving this tick AND opponent on opposite wing; `build_trigger_table()` binds SignatureIds to fn pointers); `dispatcher.rs` (`evaluate_signatures(state, slot, content_store)` iterates player's signature_candidates → loads SignatureDefinition → checks cooldown via signature_cooldowns → evaluates trigger → softmax-samples eligible candidates via `pick_top_n_softmax` with `seed_fn(match_seed, tick, SeedLayer::SignatureTrigger, (player_id << 16) | local_decision_counter)` per ADR-0009; PANICS on unknown trigger binding so content-pack drift fails loud); `bias_apply.rs` (`apply_signature_bias(raw_utility, signature_def, BiasConsideration)` multiplies the relevant SimBiasSnapshot field into utility; `BiasConsideration` enum with 6 variants including `Neutral` no-op for Idle intent); `ledger.rs` (local `MemoryEvent::SignatureFirstFired { player_slot, signature_id, tick }` stub mirroring tactic_fsm's TacticEvent pattern; T1-4 reconciles to real `MatchEvent`). `MatchState` gained 4 canonical fields: `signature_cooldowns: BTreeMap<(PlayerSlot, SignatureId), Tick>` (cooldown end-tick keyed by player+signature; BTreeMap-keyed for determinism), `signature_firing: [Option<SignatureFiring>; 22]` (per-player firing window; fixed array for deterministic encoding), `signature_first_fired_seen: BTreeSet<(PlayerSlot, SignatureId)>` (one-shot-per-match-per-pair invariant for SignatureFirstFired emission), `signature_memory_events: Vec<MemoryEvent>` with **`#[serde(skip)]`** (transient scratch buffer; cleared at top of every `dispatch_tick` call; T3-1 wires real ledger persistence; explicitly excluded from canonical encoder + pinned by `signature_memory_events_not_in_canonical_encoding` unit test). Canonical encoder VERSION 4→5; the 3 canonical signature fields appended after the ball block (signature_memory_events NOT encoded). `dispatch.rs::dispatch_tick` per-tick path: clear memory_events → iterate players via `should_decide` → call `evaluate_signatures` → if signature fires set `signature_firing[slot]` + `signature_cooldowns[(slot, id)] = tick + cooldown_ticks` + emit SignatureFirstFired (one-shot via `signature_first_fired_seen.insert`) + push to `signature_memory_events`. `subtree_library::select_outfield_intent` takes `active_bias: Option<&SimBiasSnapshot>` and composes signature bias multiplicatively AFTER personality bias at the softmax candidate-list stage (single application site — cleaner than scattering across 12 utility functions per the iii-c lesson). 3 RON signature fixtures live at `content/sources/signatures/`; `sample-am.ron` references all 3 with documented affinities. `PlayerState.signature_candidates: Vec<SignatureCandidate>` field added (`pub(crate)` + `signature_candidates()` accessor mirroring `attributes()` pattern). `intent_to_bias_consideration` enumerates every `PlayerIntent` variant explicitly (no wildcard — future intents force compile error). **Canonical hash REBASELINED per ADR-0012 trigger #1**: `blake3:1db6020c…59c798` → `blake3:18f1776c…a5d048`. Encoder VERSION progression: 1 (T0) → 2 (T1-2b-ii) → 3 (T1-2b-iii-a) → 4 (T1-2b-iii-b) → 5 (T1-2b-iv). Seventh row under the superpowers TDD mandate. **Self-review triple landed BLOCK + Revise + Revise on initial implementation** — iii-c/iii-d test-vacuousness pattern recurred in force: all 6 shipped AC tests were structurally vacuous (AC-1 tested no-op never-fires which is opposite of MEMORY criterion 1; AC-2 explicitly admitted "if cooldown exists, X holds" rather than asserting firing happens; AC-3 forced firing via direct `signature_firing[6] = Some(...)` mutation bypassing the dispatch path; AC-4 compared canonical encodings which differ from `signature_firing` field alone, not from bias multiplication; AC-5 cleared state between iterations + assertion ran vacuously if no firings; AC-6 was the only one keeping its structure). Plus 2 real semantic bugs: P0-2 `signature_memory_events` lifecycle footgun (canonical-state struct field with serde derives, NOT in encoder, NOT cleared in `tick_match`, would accumulate forever in production — every consumer using `tick_match` would silently grow this vec across the match), and P0-3 `Idle` → `Cover` wildcard silently biased softmax toward/against doing-nothing when defensive signatures were active (defensive signature's `cover_mul: 1.2` would boost Idle utility; `cover_mul: 0.5` would suppress it; user-facing semantic "defensive signature makes player more defensive" was distorted). Plus 6 P1s. **Fix pass closed all 3 P0s + 6 P1s in-place**: AC-1 split into 1a/1b/1c — three sub-tests, one per real signature, each constructing a state where the predicate IS fully satisfied + asserting the signature appears in `state.signature_firing[slot]` AND `state.signature_first_fired_seen` after running `dispatch_tick`. AC-2 forces firing once via the dispatch path + asserts `signature_first_fired_seen` stays at exactly 1 entry across 200 more ticks (the cooldown's effective semantic given the first-fired one-shot guard). AC-3 uses dispatch path with both same-category candidates eligible + asserts exactly one fires. AC-4 compares `state.players[slot].vel_x/vel_y` (the downstream effect of the softmax-pick), not full canonical encoding; uses strong-bias signature (`shoot_mul: 5.0`) to guarantee softmax-flip. AC-5 counts SignatureFirstFired emissions via natural dispatch flow across 150 ticks + asserts ≤1 per (player, signature) pair. AC-6 kept (was the only correct one). P0-2: `signature_memory_events` got `#[serde(skip)]` + cleared at top of `dispatch_tick` + new `signature_memory_events_not_in_canonical_encoding` unit test pins canonical-encoding-independence. P0-3: `BiasConsideration::Neutral` variant added + `Idle → Neutral` explicit mapping + wildcard removed from `intent_to_bias_consideration` (future intents force compile error). P1-1: `apply_signature_bias` `debug_assert!` → `assert!` for release-mode invariant on non-negative utility. P1-2: `evaluate_signatures` panics on unknown trigger binding with descriptive message (content-pack drift fails loud, not silent unwrap_or(false)). P1-3: `ron` crate moved to `[dev-dependencies]` (`fw-match-sim` consumes signature types from `fw-content` as Rust structs; never parses RON at runtime). P1-4: `SignatureFiring` fields `pub` → `pub(crate)` with `::new()` constructor + accessor methods matching `TeamTacticState` pattern. P1-5: `SignatureFiring::is_active(current_tick)` returns false explicitly when `current_tick < start_tick` (was returning true retroactively). P1-6: `PlayerState.signature_candidates` `pub` → `pub(crate)` with `signature_candidates()` accessor mirroring `attributes()` pattern. **Canonical hash UNCHANGED through the fix pass** because (a) `signature_memory_events` was never in encoder so `#[serde(skip)]` is no-op on bytes; (b) Idle→Neutral path requires non-empty `sig_definitions` which `tick_match` doesn't provide on the smoke seed — signatures don't actually fire on the smoke run, so the bias-composition difference is invisible. Two design follow-ups deferred to T2-4 systems-designer (in commit body): stacking categories for `long_range_strike` + `first_time_diagonal_switch` both being `BuildUp` prevents co-firing for any player with both candidates (intuitively a shot and a pass-switch belong in different stacking buckets); trigger threshold tuning at 0.45 vs `mid_range_baseline` 0.5 may produce too-eager firing once mid-phase Codex audit confirms. **Cross-crate dep additions (authorized)**: `fw-match-sim/Cargo.toml` gained `fw-content` as non-dev dep (consumes SignatureDefinition + SimBiasSnapshot + SignatureId types). ~1500 LoC source + ~600 LoC tests (initial implementation + fix pass combined). 14 of the previous fw-match-sim test counts + ~30 new signature-specific tests. `scripts/fw verify` green. **T1-2b sub-phase FULLY CLOSED** — match-engine inner loop complete across 8 commits since 7ae18f3 (T1-2b-i ball physics through T1-2b-iv signatures); encoder VERSION 1→5; 5 canonical-hash rebaselines; all 7 ADR-0001 architectural layers represented in canonical state. **Mid-phase Codex audit recommended** on the T1-2b accumulated diff before T1-4 begins. (this commit)
- 2026-05-15 — **T1-3 signature schema stub (`fw-content` type-system-only; no triggers yet).** Lands the signature schema lock per ADR-0011 + the per-player affinity carry-forward from FW v1's `IdentityPacket.SignatureCandidates`. New `crates/fw-content/src/signature.rs` ships the full ADR-0011 type surface: `SignatureId` newtype with `try_new` validator enforcing the Content/RULES.md §2 dotted-pack-id format (`<vendor>.<pack>[.vN]:signature.<slug>` with ≥2-segment dotted-lowercase prefix + `[a-z0-9-]+` slug; both `fwh.core:signature.no-op-stub` and `mod.community.somerset:signature.direct-run` accepted per mod-pack carve-out); `SignatureCandidate { signature_id, affinity: Q32 }` with `try_new` enforcing affinity ∈ [0,1] per ADR-0011 §"Per-player affinity"; 8-variant `RoleFamily` enum (Goalkeeper=0 through Striker=7, stable u8 discriminants for canonical encoding at T1-2b-iv); 4-variant `BiasCategory` (Attacking=0 / Defensive=1 / BuildUp=2 / SetPiece=3) per ADR-0011 §"Stacking policy"; `SimBiasSnapshot` with 5 Q32 multipliers (shoot/pass/dribble/press/cover) collapsing ADR-0003 §5's 7 personality-bias surfaces — long_pass + safe_pass merge into `pass_mul`; hold_mul dropped (hold-position is the fallback action signatures don't typically modify); the collapse rationale is documented in the type doc-comment with rollback path to 7 fields as a backwards-compat extension; `CooldownPolicy::EveryTicks(u32)` default 600 (= 10s @ 60Hz) + `CooldownPolicy::PerMatchCount(u8)` match-rare variant per ADR-0011 §"Cooldown"; `StackingPolicy::Exclusive { category: BiasCategory }`; `SignatureTrigger::NoOpStub` placeholder enum (T1-2b-iv expands with real predicate variants — `LongRangeStrike`, `BodyShieldPressure`, `FirstTimeDiagonalSwitch`); `SignaturePresentationRecipe` stub with string placeholders for `commentary_line_bank_id` + `camera_framing_hint` (T2 fills with real types); `SignatureDefinition` composes everything with schema_version: 1. `PlayerTemplate.signature_candidates: Vec<SignatureCandidate>` field added with `#[serde(default)]` (backwards-compat — existing fixtures without the field continue to load). `ContentStore.signature_definitions: BTreeMap<String, SignatureDefinition>` field; `load_sources` walks `content/sources/signatures/*.ron`. One no-op fixture at `content/sources/signatures/no-op-stub.ron` (id `fwh.core:signature.no-op-stub`); one player fixture `content/sources/players/sample-am.ron` references it with affinity 0.70. **Canonical hash UNCHANGED at `blake3:1db6020c…59c798`** — `PlayerTemplate` is template data for procedural generation; the smoke seed used by the canonical-hash regression uses hardcoded positions + `mid_range_baseline()` from MatchState::initial, NOT loaded PlayerTemplate. Schema additions are invisible to canonical match-state bytes by design (test `signature_load_does_not_drift_canonical_hash` pins this). **Data-only TDD-exempt row** per CLAUDE.md §5 mirroring T1-1's exemption; tests still ship — 14 unit tests on the new types + 5 `SignatureCandidate::try_new` validator tests + 3 load tests in `fixtures_load.rs` (signature_fixtures_load + no_op_stub_loads_via_content_store + signature_load_does_not_drift_canonical_hash). Self-review pass: type-design-analyzer returned **Revise** with 2 P1s closed in-place: P1-1 SignatureId validator was originally too loose (the agent's split-on-colon approach accepted ANY non-empty whitespace-free prefix, e.g. `Fwh.Core:signature.x` would have passed); the tightened version enforces dotted-lowercase segments ≥2 + reconciles with Content/RULES.md §2's mod-pack carve-out — rejects uppercase / underscores / single-segment / empty-segment / hyphens-in-prefix forms; adds positive tests for `fwh.core`, `fwh.core.v2`, `fwh.fantasy.elvish`, `mod.community.somerset` and negative tests for all the rejected shapes. P1-2 `SignatureCandidate::try_new` validator added with `SignatureCandidateError::AffinityOutOfRange { affinity }` error variant; tests for 0.0 / 0.5 / 1.0 success + -0.01 / 1.01 failure; previously any caller could write `Q32::from_int(5)` as affinity and skew the T1-2b-iv softmax. P3/P4 deferred to T2-3 (in commit body): `SignatureId::Deserialize` accepts arbitrary strings (validation only at `try_new`); when `ContentStore::load_baked` lands at T2-3, it should call `try_new` post-parse so typos in RON files don't silently produce unreachable signatures. Also: `SignaturePresentationRecipe` carries its own `schema_version` separate from `SignatureDefinition`'s — two schema_versions on one logical entity is a smell; reconcile at T2 when the placeholder strings become real types. **Cross-crate dep addition (authorized)**: `fw-content/Cargo.toml` gains `fw-match-sim` + `blake3` as `[dev-dependencies]` for the canonical-hash-unchanged load test. No code-side fw-match-sim dependency. ~640 LoC total across signature.rs (~370 type code) + tests (~270) + fixture RON (38). 54 unit tests now in fw-content (was 32); 33 proptest integrations across the workspace; `scripts/fw verify` clean. (this commit)
- 2026-05-15 — **T1-2b-iii-d PlayerSeparation + visual playtest gate (FINAL T1-2b-iii row).** New `crates/fw-match-sim/src/separation.rs` module ships `apply_player_separation(state: &mut MatchState)` per the FW v1 `PlayerSeparation.cs` carry-forward (design intent only; Rust idioms). Pair iteration walks 231 pairs in lex order via `for i in 0..n; for j in (i+1)..n` — structurally deterministic without HashMap/BTreeSet. Constants in Q32: `MIN_PLAYER_DISTANCE` = 0.4m, `MIN_PLAYER_DISTANCE_SQ` = 0.16m², `EPSILON_SEPARATION` ≈ 1mm. Position-only adjustment via cordic `sqrt`; velocity untouched (preserves magnitude exactly, satisfying the ±5% acceptance band trivially). Zero-distance fallback: lower-slot pushes -X, higher-slot pushes +X by EPSILON, deterministic by slot order (verified for both slots 0/1 AND slots 17/18 to pin that loop ordering doesn't reverse the rule). `tick_match` doc-comment now enumerates 6 explicit steps (tick advance → ball physics → 2 Hz heartbeat → dispatch_tick → position integration → **apply_player_separation**). 7 unit tests in separation.rs + 7 proptests in separation_proptest.rs covering all 6 MEMORY acceptance invariants 1:1: (1) isolated overlapping pair resolved; (2) non-overlapping players unchanged; (3) ball position untouched (ball_state_unchanged_by_separation unit test, gap-closed pre-eyeball); (4) velocities unchanged (exact equality — stronger than the spec's ±5%); (5) deterministic via canonical-encoding equality + zero-distance fallback pinned by slot order (gap-closed pre-eyeball); (6) tick_match wiring across a 100-tick run; (6b) "no pair stays overlapped >2 consecutive ticks across 100 ticks" — strict reading of the acceptance criterion's persistence-of-overlap clause. **Canonical hash REBASELINED per ADR-0012 trigger #1**: `blake3:235f6c5e…181288d` → `blake3:1db6020c…59c798`. Both `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs::PINNED_60_TICK` and the RON fixture's `expected_hash` updated atomically. Encoder VERSION unchanged at 4 (no schema bump; behavior-only change). 600-tick smoke fixture generated cleanly at `frontend/public/dev-fixtures/smoke-600t.json` (2.3MB, 601 frames; regenerated with `2>/dev/null` after initial cargo-stderr leak into stdout). Sixth row under the superpowers TDD mandate. **Pre-eyeball test-coverage audit caught 3 gaps post-implementation** (ball-unchanged untested; zero-distance fallback partially covered; Inv6 was 10 ticks instead of 100) — closed via a focused fix message rather than full self-review triple since the gaps were already identified via close-reading the proptest file. This is the iii-c BLOCK-verdict lesson applied prospectively: when an agent ships tests but they don't 1:1 cover acceptance, catch it BEFORE the row commits, not after. **Manual eyeball gate PASSED 2026-05-15**: user opened `/dev/board?source=fixture:/dev-fixtures/smoke-600t.json` and verbatim observed: `eyeball: PASS — players start overlapped at 4-3-3 spawn positions but fan out within a few ticks; some back-and-forth movement after separation resolves (expected skeletal-BT oscillation; iii-d's separation invariant holds)`. The back-and-forth movement is downstream of separation (skeletal-BT oscillation from identical mid_range_baseline attrs + no MatchEvent emission yet) — not a regression. Two infrastructure regressions resolved during the eyeball gate: cargo build output leaked into stdout during initial fixture generation (needed `2>/dev/null` for the regen); plus a stale vite process held port 1420 from a prior session and had to be killed before Claude Preview could manage a fresh dev server. ~200 LoC source + ~250 LoC tests. 269+ unit tests + 33 proptest integrations all green; `scripts/fw verify` clean. **T1-2b-iii sub-phase fully closed** — next is T1-2b-iv signature dispatcher (final T1-2b row). (this commit)
- 2026-05-13 — **T1-2b-iii-c BT site bindings + personality bias + utility-scored leaves.** Wires the iii-b math primitives into the iii-a BT runner. Flat `bt.rs` converted to `bt/` directory module with 4 new submodules: `personality_bias` (k₁..k₁₄ Q32 constants matching `docs/design/personality-bias-weights.md` Phase-1 seeds + 7 bias-helper functions covering all considerations from ADR-0003 §5 + `read_defender_pressure` with the 0.75 PressureTolerance divisor + `DefenderPressure` and `IsProgressive` newtype wrappers preventing argument-swap bugs); `on_ball` (7 utility scorers: shoot / pass_short / pass_long / cross / dribble / hold_ball / lay_off); `off_ball` (5 utility scorers: track_back / press / mark_player / run_off_ball / hold_formation); `reactive` (4 predicate functions — chase_loose_ball / foul_reaction / set_piece_switch / intercept_pass — defined but NOT wired into dispatch_tick, deferred to T1-4 when MatchEvent provides timing context). Each utility function reads EXACTLY the spec-mandated primary + secondary `PlayerAttributes` per `docs/specs/bt-attribute-binding.md`; bias inputs flow through the bias-helper path (not direct reads); the 3 spec field-path caveats (work_rate is personality not mental; marking is technical not mental; no goalkeeper.positioning) all correctly honored. 24 binding-correctness tests consume the per-site `*_ATTRS` const slices to enforce the spec contract under regression. `PlayerIntent` expanded 2→19 variants (Idle / MoveToPosition + 7 on-ball variants + 5 off-ball variants + 5 GK variants). `subtree_library::select_outfield_intent()` builds candidate `[(ActionId, Q32)]` lists scored by utility + applied bias for each role-state, then picks via `pick_top_n_softmax` seeded by `seed_fn(match_seed, tick, SeedLayer::UtilityTieBreak, decision_id)` per ADR-0009 (decision_id = `(player_id << 16) | local_decision_counter`). `goalkeeper_fsm::evaluate_transitions` now uses real ball-position-based predicates (ShotStopping when ball in own box moving toward goal; SweeperKeeperRush when ball past halfway with no defender between ball and goal AND opposing player approaching; DistributingFromHand when ball at GK position; InBoxPositioning default fallback) — 4 of the 5 utility-bearing GK variants are now reachable in production (PenaltyStance + SetPieceWall + Recovering defer to T1-4 when MatchEvent provides the discrete events that drive those states). `dispatch.rs::apply_intent` exhaustively handles all 19 PlayerIntent variants with explicit vel updates per variant. `tick_match` position integration uses bare panic-on-overflow operators per Codex Q1 (no `checked_mul().unwrap_or()` silent fallback). **Canonical hash REBASELINED per ADR-0012 trigger #1**: `blake3:b3b0e64f…d4da1169` → `blake3:235f6c5e…181288d` (utility outputs now drive BT-selected actions which mutate player vel non-trivially; the rebaseline captures the full behavioral change at every 4 Hz cadence tick). Atomic update to `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs::PINNED_60_TICK` + the RON fixture's `expected_hash`. Fifth row under the superpowers TDD mandate. **Self-review triple landed 4 P0 + 4 P1 + 1 P2 fixed in-place** — silent-failure-hunter initial verdict was BLOCK due to convergent contract violations: P0-1 binding-correctness drift across ~10/12 utility sites (the original implementation's `*_ATTRS` const slices were decorative; no test consumed them; ~10 sites diverged from spec including `utility_hold_formation` reading `personality.work_rate` directly — exact caveat the spec's "Implementation notes" section called out as bias-path-only). **Fix: walked every site against the spec; rewrote each utility function to read EXACTLY the documented primary + secondary attrs; shipped the load-bearing binding-correctness test suite the agent had originally punted (24 tests, one pair per site that constructs two PlayerStates differing only in declared vs non-declared attrs and asserts utility-output-changes-vs-stays-identical accordingly).** P0-2 GK FSM frozen at InBoxPositioning (the iii-a stub `evaluate_transitions` was never replaced; all 5 utility-bearing GK states existed in dispatch_state but were unreachable from `tick_goalkeeper`; 4 of 5 new `Gk*` PlayerIntent variants were silently dead production code). **Fix: implemented real ball-position-based transition predicates per ADR-0006 §"Concrete sketch"; added 10 GK predicate tests covering each state's firing condition.** P0-3 `checked_mul/add` recidivism in `lib.rs::tick_match` player position integration (reintroduced the exact iii-b purge anti-pattern: silent else branch on Q32 overflow that's mathematically impossible at MAX_PLAYER_SPEED=5/dt=1/60 ≈ 0.083). **Fix: bare panic-on-overflow operators per Codex Q1.** P0-4 `xt_delta` silently zeroing negative deltas via `checked_sub().unwrap_or(Q32::ZERO)` in `utility/xt.rs` (iii-b carryover bug surfaced by iii-c's use: backward passes became 0 xT instead of correctly-signed negative, miscalibrating `utility_pass_long`'s raw score). **Fix: bare subtraction; authorized iii-b scope expansion since the bug was surfaced by iii-c consumption.** P1-1 personality bias inputs unvalidated (`bias > 1` would propagate silently and push factor above the spec ceiling); **fix: `debug_assert!` on bias input ranges in all 7 helpers.** P1-2 `select_outfield_intent` silent fallback to `MoveToPosition` on empty candidates (convergent with type-design F2); **fix: `unreachable!()` matching the GK panic discipline at subtree_library.rs:162.** P1-3 the punted `utility_site_proptest.rs` was load-bearing for P0-1 — shipped as the 24 binding-correctness unit tests directly. P1-4 RNG seed-layer mismatch in dispatch.rs (`SeedLayer::Decision` used where ADR-0009 specifies `UtilityTieBreak` for softmax tie-breaking); **fix: corrected layer; this DID change the canonical hash because RNG byte output differs per layer discriminant, captured in the same rebaseline.** P2-2 `DefenderPressure` + `IsProgressive` newtype wrappers prevent argument-swap bugs in `apply_shoot_bias` (raw, attrs, defender_pressure) vs `apply_long_pass_bias` (raw, attrs, is_progressive) where both third args were raw Q32 of identical type but completely different semantics. Two type-design follow-ups deferred to iii-d / T1-4: F1 `PlayerIntent` 19-variant explosion → `Target` struct collapse (18 of 19 variants carry identical `{ target_x, target_y }`; refactor touches every match arm; defer); F5 GK return-type constraint (separate `GkIntent` subset enum so only the 5 GK variants can flow from goalkeeper_fsm). **Lesson captured:** the proptest deferral pattern was directly responsible for the BLOCK verdict — when an agent punts a test, the punt's reasoning needs adversarial review BEFORE the row commits, not after. ~1500 LoC source (initial + fix pass); 67 new tests across the new modules; `scripts/fw verify` clean. (this commit)
- 2026-05-13 — **T1-2b-iii-b utility math primitives + PlayerAttributes baseline.** Pure-function Q32 implementation of ADR-0003 §1-§6: five new modules in `fw-match-sim::utility` (`xg` 6-feature logistic with third-pass β values from `docs/design/xg-coefficients.md` + `ShotContext::try_new()` validator + sanity checks at 3 canonical reference points; `xt` 192-entry hand-authored `XT_GRID: [Q32; 192]` + `PitchZone` newtype + `xt_delta()`; `pitch_control` Spearman closed-form per-point time-to-intercept + `PitchControlOutcome` with `attacker + defender + neutral == 1` by construction; `pressing` Bauer 2025 product form; `softmax` top-N selection via exp_q32 + ChaCha8Rng seeded through `seed_fn(match_seed, tick, SeedLayer::UtilityTieBreak, decision_id)` per ADR-0009; returns `Option<ActionId>`). New `fw-core::math` module (cross-crate scope expansion authorized per ADR-0003 §1 — sibling crates will need the same LUTs): 257-entry symmetric Q32 LUTs for sigmoid + exp over `[-8, +8]` with linear interpolation; f64 confined to one-shot `LazyLock` bake at process startup, pure-Q32 `lut_eval` interpolation at runtime. `Q32` substrate gained `to_raw` alias, `acos` via cordic (unconditional `assert!` matching `sqrt` precedent), `from_f64_clamped` (`pub(crate)` — LUT-bake-only escape, never per-tick). `PlayerAttributes::mid_range_baseline()` constructor in `fw-core` sets all 55 fields to Q32 ≈ 0.5; `mid_range_baseline_is_in_unit_range` test catches future drift between baseline + `validate_unit_range`. `PlayerState` gained `attributes: PlayerAttributes` field (`pub(crate)` + public `attributes()` accessor mirroring iii-a's `local_decision_counter` pattern). Canonical encoder VERSION bumped 3→4; emits 55 attribute Q32 fields per player after the existing per-player surface (+9680 bytes per match-state encoding). `tick_match` UNCHANGED structurally — math modules exist as pure functions exposed for T1-2b-iii-c to wire into BT site bindings. **Canonical hash REBASELINED per ADR-0012 trigger #1**: `blake3:c0b5e395…c1430ff` → `blake3:b3b0e64f…d4da1169`. Atomic update to `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs::PINNED_60_TICK` + the RON fixture's `expected_hash`. Fourth row under the superpowers TDD mandate; RED-GREEN-REFACTOR observed per chunk. **Self-review triple landed 4 P0 + 9 P1 + 3 P2 fixed in-place** — silent-failure-hunter initially returned BLOCK on a serious determinism finding: P0-1 `math.rs::lut_eval` was running f64 arithmetic per-tick (despite the `from_f64_clamped` doc-comment contract claiming bake-time-only use), putting f64 in the canonical critical path and breaking ADR-0003's bit-exact-across-platforms promise. **Fix: rewrote `lut_eval` in pure Q32** — the index calculation `(x + 8) * 16` is a Q32 left-shift-by-4 with no rounding; the fractional part is the low 32 bits of the Q32 representation directly; the interpolation is pure-Q32 arithmetic. The hash stayed bit-identical through this fix because LUT entries are stored as Q32 (baked once in f64 then frozen), and the Q32 index arithmetic agrees with the f64 path for all in-domain inputs. P0-2 replaced all `checked_*().unwrap_or()` silent-failure patterns across xg/pitch_control/pressing/softmax with bare panic-on-overflow operators per Codex Q1 — invariants are now load-bearing tripwires instead of swallowing-fallbacks. P0-3 `Q32::acos` `debug_assert!` → `assert!` (same release-stripped anti-pattern that bit iii-a's `formation_position`). P0-4 missing 12-yard central xG canonical sanity-check test added per `xg-coefficients.md` row 2; xG bands tightened (30m long shot: [1.5%, 5%]; penalty: [58%, 72%]). P1-1 `PitchControlOutcome` gained `neutral_control: Q32` so the sum-to-1 invariant holds by construction (previously each control side was clamped independently; lost mass disappeared silently). P1-4 `crates/fw-match-sim/tests/utility_proptest.rs` added with 7 invariants covering all 5 utility modules — closed the TDD-mandate gap from the original implementation. P1-5 `ShotContext::try_new + ShotContextError` mirroring AbilityCeiling pattern; fields `pub(crate)`. P1-6 `Q32::from_f64_clamped` pub → pub(crate). P1-7 `XT_GRID` pub → pub(crate). P1-8 `pick_top_n_softmax` returns `Option<ActionId>` and drops `Default` bound — eliminates the ActionId=0 collision risk. P1-9 misleading `PlayerSnapshot` "borrowed from canonical state" doc-comment removed. P2-1 `PlayerState::attributes` pub → pub(crate) + `attributes()` accessor. P2-2 `q32.rs` `EXEMPT_FILES` exemption rescoped narrowly post-P1-6 lockdown. P2-3 `mid_range_baseline_is_in_unit_range` sync test added. P3 deferred to follow-up rows: `determinism-audit.py` cfg(any(test, feature)) regex coverage gap; `pitch_control` Vec allocation per call (T1-2c performance pass). **Cross-crate scope expansions explicitly authorized by user 2026-05-13** alongside the iii-b/c/d further-split: new `fw-core::math` module; new `Q32` methods (`to_raw`/`acos`/`from_f64_clamped`); `scripts/determinism-audit.py` cfg(test)-block stripping pass + narrow `EXEMPT_FILES` additions for `math.rs` LUT-bake path + `q32.rs` `from_f64_clamped` (post-P1-6 the `q32.rs` exemption is itself a candidate for removal once test-only call sites are refactored to use a cfg(test)-gated helper — captured as a T1-3 cleanup). 255 unit tests + 26 proptest integrations; `scripts/fw verify` clean.
