- 2026-05-13 — **T1-2b-iii-a `fw-match-sim` BT runner + per-role BT skeletons (skeleton tier per ADR-0006).** Five new modules: `bt` (Tree / Node / NodeStatus + Selector/Sequence/Decorator/Leaf/Condition variants + ordered Vec child traversal), `role_states` (`Role` 4-variant + per-role state enums GK/DEF/MID/FWD ~7-8 variants each + `PlayerRoleState` typed-pair enum atomically coupling role+state + `PlayerIntent`), `subtree_library` (hardcoded 4-3-3 stub library — every leaf returns `MoveToFormationPosition`; FORMATION_4_3_3_POSITIONS const), `goalkeeper_fsm` (pure Rust functions per GK state per ADR-0006 §"Concrete sketch"), `dispatch` (dispatch_tick + apply_intent + preempt_check stub + evaluate_transitions stub). `PlayerState` gained `role_state: PlayerRoleState` (replaces the prior split `role: Role` + `role_state: u8` with atomic typed pairing — illegal pairs unrepresentable per type-design self-review P1) + `local_decision_counter: u32` (`pub(crate)` + `decision_counter()` accessor + crate-internal `bump_decision_counter()`). Canonical encoder VERSION bumped 2→3; wire-format diagram extended to show the new per-player section ordering (`role u8 → role_state u8 → local_decision_counter u32 LE` appended after scalars; +6 bytes × 22 = +132 bytes per match-state). `tick_match` is now the complete-ish per-tick loop: (1) ball physics integration, (2) tactic-FSM 2 Hz heartbeat, (3) `dispatch_tick` iterating 22 roster slots gated by `should_decide` (from T1-2b-ii) calling either GK FSM or outfield BT, (4) **player position integration from velocity** (P0 self-review fix — `apply_intent` was setting vel but tick_match never advanced pos; without it the sim modeled nothing). Roles assigned by slot per default 4-3-3 (1 GK + 4 DEF + 3 MID + 3 FWD per side); home slots 0..11, away slots 11..22. **Canonical hash REBASELINED per ADR-0012 trigger #1** (canonical schema bump — PlayerState +3 fields, encoder VERSION 2→3): `blake3:5aea582b…cf5c544` → `blake3:c0b5e395…c1430ff`. Atomic update to `crates/fw-replay/tests/canonical_hash.rs::PINNED_60_TICK` AND the RON fixture's `expected_hash`. Third row under the superpowers TDD mandate; RED-GREEN-REFACTOR observed per chunk. **Self-review triple landed 1 P0 + 4 P1 + 3 P2 fixed in-place** (the P0 was player position integration — promoted from silent-failure-hunter's borderline "if intentional, document; if not, P0" because the sim genuinely models nothing without it; the load-bearing P1 was the PlayerRoleState typed-pair collapse, subsuming a silent-failure-hunter P1 on `from_tag().unwrap_or()`; remaining P1s: `SubtreeLibrary::lookup_outfield` made infallible with clear panic on miss, `local_decision_counter` visibility tightened to mirror T1-2b-ii's TeamTacticState pattern, outfield `evaluate_transitions` step added before BT lookup per ADR-0006 §"Concrete sketch"; P2s: `formation_position` unconditional `assert!` replacing release-stripped `debug_assert!` + `.min()` clamp, misleading `gk_slot0_moves_toward_goal_line` test renamed + honest movement-verifying replacement added, `roster_slot` 1-indexed vs 0-indexed naming disambiguated to `roster_slot` / `formation_slot`). The PlayerRoleState collapse is byte-identical to the prior split-fields encoding so the canonical hash stayed stable through the self-review pass; position integration is zero-displacement at tick 60 because all 22 players start AT their formation positions, so MoveToFormationPosition produces vel=0 in skeleton tier (the rebaseline thus captures the schema bump alone, not behavior drift). Known follow-ups deferred to -iii-b/-iii-c: `PlayerState::at` defaulting role to Midfielder (footgun for old call sites bypassing `with_role`), `PlayerIntent::priority` field for utility merge, `LeafKind::Idle`/`PlayerIntent::Idle` redundancy, explicit wire-format insta snapshot. ~880 LoC source + ~470 LoC tests + ~150 LoC self-review refactor. 138 unit tests + 19 proptest integrations all green; `scripts/fw verify` clean. ## Phase T0: Scaffold — closed 2026-05-13 (preliminary; superseded by APPROVED block above)

**Shipped:** an empty repo became a workspace-verifying Rust+Tauri+SolidJS scaffold with deterministic match-sim primitives, a pinned BLAKE3 canonical-state hash, a reconciled blueprint, a 9-step `/next` workflow, and Codex pre-T0 audit findings landed.

- **Crates (8 + Tauri shell)**: `fw-core` (Q32.32 panic-on-overflow + Seed + Tick + durable u32 IDs), `fw-match-sim` (22-player struct + canonical encoder + tick reducer), `fw-content` (schema + integer-bps weights), `fw-content-baker` (CLI scaffold with staged prompts/schemas/validators), `fw-scouting` (stub), `fw-memory` (MemoryEvent stub with Q32 stakes/salience), `fw-replay` (pinned-hash regression test + RON fixture + sanity-non-zero guard + corpus-agreement test), `fw-save` (SaveV1 enum stub), `fw-tauri` (DTOs + IPC handlers in sibling commands module), `src-tauri` (Tauri shell + frontend/dist stub + icon stubs).
- **Frontend**: Vite + SolidJS + Tailwind v3 + TanStack v8 + PixiJS v8 + ECharts scaffolded with 6 placeholder routes.
- **Workflow**: 7-agent roster, 6 slash commands, 5 path-scoped rule modules, 5 hooks, 3 context scopes, 8 design templates, 9 patterns.
- **Determinism gate**: pinned BLAKE3 = `d6258107b2c90c84d2feeaa8633d1f5c159e10ccd2016623b52b41d3d96b1a49` on the smoke seed at tick 60. Bit-identical across 100 fresh runs intra-process on macOS-14. Cross-OS matrix agreement (T0-7b) verified by the phase-gate PR's CI.
- **Codex pre-T0 audit**: 14 of 16 findings fixed in-tree; 2 intentionally deferred (src-tauri command consolidation → T1-5; bincode dev-dep alignment → T2-9). 7 of 7 open questions resolved.
- **Canonical-state hash:** `blake3:d6258107…` (60-tick smoke seed).
- **Tests:** 19 test-runs across 13 crates + integration; all green on macOS-14 dev box. Cross-OS verification pending T0-7b.
- **Decisions logged:** 0 in `docs/DECISIONS.md` this phase (architectural decisions were made via STATUS open-questions resolution + Codex audit follow-ups, all documented in the commit bodies). First DECISIONS entry expected at T1 (BT-runner contract).
